Where the act committed by the person not criminally responsible corresponds to a crime related to the use of a weapon, the court may order the revocation of the weapon use and carry licence for a period of 5 to 10 years, whenever that person's personality gives rise to well-founded fear that new acts of the same kind will be committed.

3. The provisions of paragraph 3 of Article 86 apply correspondingly.

##### CHAPTER IX

OTHER CONSEQUENCES OF CRIME

###### Article 102

Forfeiture of objects of the crime

1. Objects which served or were intended to serve for the commission of a crime, or which were produced thereby, are declared forfeited to the State when by their nature or the circumstances of the case they endanger the safety of persons or public order, or present serious risks of being used for the commission of new crimes.
2. The rights of the victim and of third parties who did not contribute to their use or production, or derive advantage from the object of which they are owners, are safeguarded.
3. The court determines the destination of objects declared forfeited whenever the law does not do so, and may order their total or partial destruction or place them outside commerce.
4. The provisions of paragraph 1 apply even if no specific person can be punished for the act.

###### Article 103 Forfeiture of advantages

1. All things, rights or advantages acquired, directly or indirectly, as a consequence of the commission of a crime, are declared forfeited to the State, without prejudice to the rights of the victim or third parties in good faith.
2. If the things, rights or advantages cannot be appropriated in kind, the forfeiture is replaced by payment to the State of their value.

###### Article 104 Civil liability arising from crime

1. Compensation for losses and damages arising from a crime is mandatory and officially determined and awarded by the court whenever the damages have been established and quantified, unless the injured party declares under the terms of criminal procedural law that it intends to bring the claim separately.
2. The requirements and calculation of compensation are governed by the rules of civil law.
3. The person liable for compensation may settle it by giving notice to the court, on pain of ineffectiveness of the act.

###### Article 105 Privilege of the injured party's claim

The claim arising from the injured party's right to compensation for losses and damages arising from crime enjoys preference over any other arising after the commission of the act, including costs and the amount relating to the fine.

#### TITLE V RIGHT TO LODGE A COMPLAINT

###### Article 106 Nature of the crime

1. Crimes may be of a public or semi-public nature, for the purposes of exercising the right to lodge a complaint.
2. Public crimes are those whose criminal proceedings do not depend on a complaint.
3. Semi-public crimes are those whose proceedings can only be initiated after the right to lodge a complaint has been exercised.
4. The right to lodge a complaint consists in the manifestation of will by the holder thereof that criminal proceedings are desired.

###### Article 107

Holder of the right to lodge a complaint

When criminal proceedings depend on a complaint, those entitled to lodge it are the holders of the right indicated in criminal procedural law.

###### Article 108

Time limit for exercising the right

The time limit for exercising the right to lodge a complaint is 6 months and runs independently for each of the holders of the right to lodge a complaint.

###### Article 109

Waiver and withdrawal of complaint

The waiver, withdrawal or non-exercise of the right to lodge a complaint in relation to one of the participants in the crime benefits the others, in cases where they too cannot be prosecuted without a complaint.

#### TITLE VI

EXTINCTION OF CRIMINAL LIABILITY

##### CHAPTER I

PRESCRIPTION OF CRIMINAL PROCEEDINGS

###### Article 110 Prescription periods

1. Criminal proceedings are extinguished by prescription as soon as the following periods have elapsed since the commission of the crime:
a) 20 years, in the case of crimes punishable by imprisonment whose maximum limit exceeds 12 years;
b) 15 years, in the case of crimes punishable by imprisonment whose maximum limit exceeds 7 years, but does not exceed 12 years;
c) 8 years, in the case of crimes punishable by imprisonment whose maximum limit exceeds 3 years, but does not exceed 7 years;
d) 4 years, in all other cases.

2. When the law establishes for any crime, in the alternative, imprisonment or a fine, only the former is considered for the purposes of the provisions of this article.

###### Article 111 Calculation of the period

1. The prescription period for criminal proceedings runs from the day on which the act was consummated or from the day of the last act of execution in the case of an unconsummated crime, continuing crime or habitual crime.
2. In permanent crimes the prescription period is counted from the day on which the consummation ceases.
3. In the case of complicity, regard is had to the act of the perpetrator.

###### Article 112 Suspension of prescription

1. Prescription of criminal proceedings is suspended, in addition to cases specially provided for by law, during the time in which:
a) The proceedings cannot legally be initiated or continued for lack of legal authorisation or a judgment to be delivered by a non-criminal court, or by reason of the referral of a preliminary question to non-criminal proceedings;
b) The offender is serving, abroad, a sentence or custodial security measure;
c) Criminal proceedings are pending, from the moment the accused is notified of the indictment.
2. Prescription resumes running from the day on which the cause of suspension ceases.
3. The cause of suspension cannot, depending on the circumstances, exceed half the period provided for in Article 110.

##### CHAPTER II PRESCRIPTION OF SENTENCES AND SECURITY

MEASURES

###### Article 113

Prescription periods for sentences
1. Sentences are prescribed in the following periods:
a) 25 years if they exceed 12 years of imprisonment;
b) 20 years if they exceed 8 years but do not exceed 12 years of imprisonment;
c) 12 years if they exceed 4 years but do not exceed 8 years of imprisonment;
d) 8 years in the remaining cases of imprisonment sentences;
e) 4 years in the case of fines.
2. The prescription period for sentences is counted from the date on which the decision imposing it becomes final.

###### Article 114 Prescription of accessory sentences

Prescription of accessory sentences is subject to the regime of the principal sentence.

###### Article 115

Prescription periods for security measures

Security measures are prescribed in the following cases:
a) 15 years if custodial;
b) 5 years if non-custodial;
c) 2 years in cases of revocation of weapon carry licence.

###### Article 116 Suspension of prescription

1. Prescription of sentences and security measures is suspended, in addition to cases specially provided for by law, during the time in which:
a) By force of law execution cannot begin or continue;
b) After the escape of the convicted person and while not recaptured;
c) The convicted person is serving another sentence or custodial security measure;
d) Deferment of payment of the fine continues;

e) The convicted person is temporarily prevented from performing community service;
f) Execution is taking place.
2. Prescription resumes running from the day on which the cause of suspension ceases.
3. The provisions of paragraph 3 of Article 112 apply correspondingly.

##### CHAPTER III IMPRESCRIPTIBILITY

###### Article 117

Crimes of genocide, against peace and humanity and war crimes

Criminal proceedings and penalties imposed for crimes of genocide, against peace and humanity and war crimes are imprescriptible.

##### CHAPTER IV

OTHER CAUSES OF EXTINCTION

###### Article 118 Other causes

In addition to cases specially provided for by law, criminal liability is extinguished by the death of the offender, by amnesty and by pardon.

###### Article 119 Death of the offender

The death of the offender extinguishes criminal proceedings, as well as the criminal sanction that has been imposed on them.

###### Article 120 Amnesty

Amnesty extinguishes criminal proceedings and terminates the execution of the sanction not yet served in whole or in part, as well as its effects and accessory penalties to the extent possible.

###### Article 121 Amnesty and concurrence of crimes

Unless otherwise provided, amnesty is applicable to each of the crimes that constitute the concurrence.

###### Article 122 Pardon

Pardon extinguishes the penalty, in whole or in part, or substitutes it with another provided for by law and more favourable to the convicted person.

## BOOK II SPECIAL PART

#### TITLE I

CRIMES AGAINST PEACE AND HUMANITY

##### CHAPTER I

CRIMES OF GENOCIDE AND AGAINST HUMANITY

###### Article 123 Genocide

1.	Whoever, with intent to destroy, in whole or in part, a national, ethnic, racial or religious group, commits:
a)	Homicide or serious physical or psychological injury to members of the group;
b)	Acts that by any means prevent procreation or the birth of members of the group;
c)	Rape, sexual slavery, forced prostitution, forced pregnancy, forced sterilization or any other form of sexual violence of comparable gravity;
d)	Forcible separation of members of the group to another group;
e)	Acts that violently prevent the group from settling or remaining in a geographical area that

is recognized as theirs by tradition or historically;
f)	Subjecting the group to conditions of existence or to cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment, likely to bring about its destruction, in whole or in part;
g)	Widespread confiscation or seizure of property belonging to members of the group;
h)	Prohibition of certain commercial, industrial or professional activities to members of the group;
i)	Spreading of an epidemic likely to cause death or physical injury to members of the group;
j)	Prohibition, omission or prevention by any means of the provision to members of the group of humanitarian assistance adequate to combat situations of epidemic or severe food shortage;
shall be punished with a penalty of 15 to 30 years' imprisonment.
2.	Whoever publicly and directly incites the commission of genocide shall be punished with a penalty of 5 to 15 years' imprisonment.

###### Article 124

Crimes against humanity

Whoever, in the context of a widespread or systematic attack against any civilian population, commits acts resulting in:
a)	Homicide or serious physical or psychological injury;
b)	Extermination, understood as the subjection of all or part of the population to adverse living conditions, such as deprivation of access to food or medicine, capable of causing the death of one or more persons;
c)	Enslavement;
d)	Deportation or forcible transfer of a population, understood as the unlawful displacement of one or more persons to another State or location through their expulsion or other coercive act;

e)	Imprisonment or any other severe form of deprivation of physical liberty of a person, in violation of the norms or principles of international law;
f)	Torture, understood as the act consisting of inflicting severe pain or suffering, physical or psychological, on a person deprived of liberty or under the control of the offender;
g)	Rape, sexual slavery, forced prostitution, forced pregnancy, forced sterilization or any other form of sexual violence of comparable gravity;
h)	Persecution, understood as the deprivation of the enjoyment of fundamental rights, in violation of international law, of a group or collectivity that can be identified on political, racial, national, ethnic, cultural, religious, gender grounds or on the basis of other grounds universally recognized as unacceptable under international law;
i)	Enforced disappearance of persons, understood as the arrest, detention or abduction promoted by a State or political organization, or with its authorization, support or acquiescence, followed by refusal to acknowledge such state of deprivation of liberty or to provide any information on the situation or location of those persons, with the purpose of denying them the protection of the law for a prolonged period of time;
j)	Apartheid, understood as any inhuman act committed in the context of an institutionalized regime of systematic oppression and domination by one racial group over another or others, with the intention of maintaining that regime;
k)	Other inhuman acts of a similar character intentionally causing great suffering, serious injury or affecting mental or physical health;
shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of 15 to 30 years.

##### CHAPTER II

WAR CRIMES

###### Article 125

War crimes against persons

1.	Whoever, in the context of an armed conflict of an international character or armed conflict of a non-international character, against a person protected by international humanitarian law, commits:
a)	Homicide;
b)	Torture or cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment including biological experiments;
c)	Serious physical injury or acts causing great suffering;
d)	Taking of hostages;
e)	Compelling to serve in enemy armed forces or proceeding to recruit or enlist children under 18 years of age in armed forces, military or paramilitary forces of a State, or in armed groups distinct from the armed forces, military or paramilitary forces of a State, or their use to participate in hostilities;
f)	Serious, prolonged and unjustified restrictions on the liberty of persons;
g)	Deportation or transfer, or unlawful deprivation of liberty;
h)	Unjustified removal or destruction of property of great value;
i)	Conviction and execution of sentence, without prior fair and impartial trial;
j)	Acts that outrage the dignity of the human person, in particular by means of humiliating and degrading treatment;
k)	Homicide or injuries inflicted on a combatant who has laid down arms or who, having no means to defend himself, has unconditionally surrendered or has in any way been placed out of combat;
l)	The acts described in paragraph g) of the preceding article;

m)	Subjecting persons who are under the control of a belligerent party to physical mutilations or to any type of medical or scientific experiments that are not motivated by medical, dental or hospital treatment, nor carried out in the interest of those persons, and that cause death or seriously endanger their health;
shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of 12 to 25 years.
2.	The penalty is increased by one fifth in its limits when the acts referred to in the preceding paragraph are committed against members of a humanitarian institution.
3.	Whoever, in the context of an armed conflict of an international character:
a)	Transfers, directly or indirectly, as an occupying power, part of its own civilian population into the occupied territory or transfers all or part of the population of the occupied territory, within or outside that territory;
b)	Compels a prisoner of war or other protected person to serve in the armed forces of an enemy power;
c)	After the cessation of hostilities, delays, without justified reason, the repatriation of prisoners of war;
shall be punished with a penalty of imprisonment of 15 to 30 years.

###### Artigo 126º

War crimes by use of prohibited methods of warfare

Whoever, in the context of an armed conflict of an international character or armed conflict of a non-international character:
a) Attacks the civilian population as a whole or civilians not taking direct part in hostilities;
b) Attacks civilian objects, that is, objects that are not military objectives;
c) Attacks, by any means, population centres, dwellings or buildings that are not

defended and that are not military objectives;
d) Launches an indiscriminate attack affecting the civilian population or civilian objects, knowing that such attack will cause loss of human life, injury to civilians or damage to civilian objects, which are excessive;
e) Takes advantage of the presence of civilians or other protected persons to prevent certain points, areas or military forces from being the object of military operations;
f) Deliberately causes starvation of the civilian population as a method of warfare, depriving it of objects indispensable to its survival;
g) Declares or threatens, in the capacity of an officer, that no quarter will be given;
h) Kills or wounds treacherously enemy combatants;
i) Launches an attack, being able to know that it will cause widespread, long-term and severe damage to the environment which is clearly excessive in relation to the concrete and direct overall military advantage anticipated;
j) Commits perfidy, understood as the act of killing, wounding or capturing, which appeals, with intent to deceive, to the good faith of an adversary to make him believe that he has the right to receive, or the obligation to ensure the protection provided by the rules of international humanitarian law;
is punished with imprisonment from 15 to 30 years.

###### Artigo 127º

War crimes by use of prohibited means of warfare

1. Whoever, in the context of an armed conflict of an international character or an armed conflict of a non-international character, employs weapons, projectiles, materials and methods of combat which, by their very nature, cause superfluous injury or unnecessary suffering or which have indiscriminate effects,

in violation of international law applicable to armed conflicts, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.
2. The preceding paragraph covers, in particular, the use of:
a) Poison or poisoned weapons;
b) Asphyxiating, toxic or similar gases or any analogous liquid, material or device;
c) Bullets which expand or flatten easily in the human body, such as bullets with a hard casing which does not entirely cover the core or has incisions;
d) Anti-personnel mines;
e) Chemical weapons;
f) Weapons the primary effect of which is to injure by fragments not detectable by X-rays in the human body;
g) Incendiary weapons;
h) Laser weapons causing blindness.
3. The weapons, instruments and products referred to in the preceding paragraph are those which are considered as such by international law.

###### Artigo 128º

War crimes against property protected by distinctive insignia or emblems

Whoever, in the context of an armed conflict of an international character or armed conflict of a non-international character, attacks:
a) Personnel, installations, material, units or vehicles participating in a peacekeeping or humanitarian assistance mission, in accordance with the Charter of the United Nations, provided that they are entitled to the protection given to civilians or civilian objects under international humanitarian law;
b) Buildings, installations, material, units or vehicles, duly marked with the distinctive emblems of the Geneva Conventions

or personnel authorized to use the same emblems.
is punished with imprisonment from 10 to 20 years.

###### Artigo 129º

War crimes against property

Whoever, in the context of an international armed conflict or in the context of an armed conflict of a non-international character:
a) Appropriates, destroys or damages property on a large scale or of great value, without military necessity or in an unlawful and arbitrary manner;
b) Attacks, destroys or damages buildings dedicated to religious worship, education, the arts, sciences or charitable purposes, cultural or historical monuments, archaeological sites, hospitals and places where the sick and wounded are collected, provided that they are not military objectives;
c) Pillages a town or place, even when taken by assault;
is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 130º

War crimes against other rights

Whoever, in the context of an international armed conflict or in the context of an armed conflict of a non-international character, declares abolished, suspended or inadmissible in a court of law, any rights and proceedings of nationals of the adverse party is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

##### CAPÍTULO III AGAINST PEACE AND FREEDOM

###### Artigo 131º Terrorist organizations

1. A terrorist group, organization or association is considered to be a grouping of two or more persons who, in order to achieve political, ideological, philosophical or religious purposes, act in concert, aiming to harm national integrity or independence, prevent, alter or subvert the functioning of national or international institutions, intimidate or compel public authority, international organizations or certain persons, groups of persons or the general population, to perform an act, to abstain from performing it or to tolerate that it be performed, through the commission of serious crimes:
a) Against the life, physical integrity or freedom of persons;
b) Against the security of transport and communications, including telegraphic, telephonic, radio or television;
c) Of intentional production of common danger, through fire, explosion, release of radioactive substances or toxic or asphyxiating gases, flood or avalanche, collapse of construction, contamination of food and water intended for human consumption or spread of disease, plague, harmful plant or animal;
d) That involve the use of nuclear energy, firearms, explosive substances or devices, incendiary means of any nature, booby-trapped parcels or letters.
e) Acts that destroy or make impossible the functioning or divert from their normal purposes, permanently or temporarily, in whole or in part, means or ways of communication, public service facilities or international facilities or facilities intended for the supply and satisfaction of the vital needs of the population.
f) Research and development of biological or chemical weapons
2. Whoever promotes or founds a terrorist group, organization or association, joins, participates in or supports them, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.

3. Whoever leads or directs a terrorist group, organization or association is punished with imprisonment from 15 to 30 years.
4. When a terrorist group, organization or association, or the persons referred to in paragraphs 2 or 3, possess any of the means indicated in paragraph 1(d), the penalty is increased by one third in its minimum and maximum limits.
5. Preparatory acts for the establishment of a terrorist group, organization or association are punished with the penalty reduced by half in its minimum and maximum limits.
6. The penalties referred to may be extraordinarily mitigated or punishment may not take place if the agent voluntarily abandons his activity, prevents or seriously endeavours to prevent the danger caused by it or the continuation of terrorist groups, organizations or associations, or communicates their existence to the authority so that it can prevent the commission of crimes.

###### Article 132 Terrorism

1. Whoever commits any of the crimes provided for in paragraphs a) to c) and e) of number 1 of the preceding article, or any crime using the means referred to in paragraphs d) or f) of the same provision, with the intention referred to therein, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years, or with the penalty corresponding to the crime committed, increased by one third in its minimum and maximum limits, if the penalty is equal to or greater.
2. The penalty may be extraordinarily mitigated or punishment may not take place if the agent voluntarily abandons his activity, removes or considerably reduces the danger caused by it, prevents the result that the law seeks to avoid from occurring, or concretely assists in the collection of decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other responsible parties.

###### Article 133 Financing of terrorism

Whoever by any means, directly or indirectly and intentionally, provides, collects or holds funds or property of any kind, as well as products or rights capable of being transformed into funds, attempts to do so, with the intention that they be used or knowing that they may be used, in whole or in part, in the planning, preparation or practice of the acts provided for in number 1 of article 131, or commits these acts with the intention referred to in number 1 of article 132, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.

###### Article 134 Incitement to war

1. Whoever, by any means, publicly and repeatedly, incites hatred against a race, a people or a nation, with the intention of provoking a war or preventing peaceful coexistence between different races, peoples or nations, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2. Whoever entices or recruits Timorese or foreign citizens to, in the service of a foreign group or power, wage war against a State or to overthrow the legitimate Government of another State by violent means, is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

###### Article 135 Racial or religious discrimination

1. Whoever founds, constitutes an organization or develops organized propaganda activities that incite or encourage racial or religious discrimination, hatred or violence, as well as whoever participates in or provides assistance, including its financing, to the organization or the activities referred to, is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.
2. Whoever, in a public meeting, by writing intended for dissemination or through any means of social communication, disseminates ideas with the intention of inciting or encouraging racial or religious discrimination, or provokes acts of violence against a person or group of

persons because of their race, colour, ethnic origin or religion, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

##### CHAPTER IV COMMON PROVISIONS

###### Article 136

Responsibility of military commanders and other superiors

1. The military commander or person acting as such who, having knowledge that the forces under his effective command and control or under his effective responsibility and control are committing any of the crimes provided for in this title, does not adopt all necessary and adequate measures to prevent or suppress their commission or to bring it to the immediate knowledge of the competent authorities, is punished with the penalty corresponding to the crime or crimes that are effectively committed.
2. The provisions of the preceding number are applicable, with due adaptations, to the superior regarding the control of subordinates under his effective authority and control.

###### Article 137 Definitions

For the purposes of this Title, the following are considered:
a) "Armed conflict of an international character", one that:
i) Occurs between States, even without a formal declaration of war, even if the state of war is not recognized by one of them;
ii) Corresponds to a situation of total or partial occupation of the territory of a State, even if that occupation does not encounter any military resistance;
iii) Is subsumed in a situation in which peoples fight against colonial domination, foreign occupation and against segregation regimes, in the exercise of the right of peoples to self-determination,

enshrined in the Charter of the United Nations and in the declaration concerning the principles of international law with regard to friendly relations and cooperation among States;
b) "Armed conflict of a non-international character", one that takes place in the territory of a State, is of a prolonged nature and opposes governmental authorities and organized armed groups or these among themselves, with the exception of situations of internal disturbances and tensions, such as sporadic or isolated acts of violence or others of a similar nature;
c) "Protected persons":
i) In international armed conflicts, persons protected for the purposes of the Geneva Conventions of 1949 and Additional Protocol I, namely the wounded, sick, shipwrecked, prisoners of war, medical or religious personnel and civilian population;
ii) In armed conflict of a non-international character, the wounded, the sick, the shipwrecked, as well as persons not taking active part in hostilities in the power of the enemy;
iii) In armed conflict of an international character and in armed conflict of a non-international character, members of the armed forces and combatants of the enemy party who have laid down their arms or have no other means of defence.

#### TITLE II

CRIMES AGAINST PERSONS

##### CHAPTER I AGAINST LIFE

###### Article 138 Simple homicide

Whoever kills another person is punished with imprisonment from 8 to 20 years.

###### Article 139 Aggravated homicide

If death is produced in circumstances that reveal special culpability or perversity, namely:
a) With the use of poison, torture, asphyxiation, fire, explosive or other insidious means or that results in the commission of a crime of common danger, or, further, with another act of cruelty to increase the suffering of the victim;

b) By means of dissimulation or other means or recourse that makes defence by the victim difficult or impossible;
c) Out of greed, for the pleasure of killing, for excitement or to satisfy sexual instinct, by payment or reward or its promise, or for any other futile or base motive;
d) With the purpose of preparing, executing or covering up another crime, facilitating the escape or ensuring the impunity of the agent of a crime;
e) Out of racial, religious or political hatred;
f) With premeditation, this being understood as coldness of mind, reflection on the means employed or postponement of the intention to kill for more than 24 hours;
g) If the victim is the spouse, descendant, ascendant, collateral, related family member up to the second degree, adoptee of the agent or person who lives with him in analogous conditions where there is a hierarchical, economic or work dependence;
h) If the victim is a particularly vulnerable person by reason of age, illness or physical or mental disability;
i) If the victim is a member of a sovereign body and constitutional political bodies, member of a local administration body, magistrate,

defender, lawyer, court officer, official or any other person charged with a public service, provided that it is in the exercise or because of the exercise of their functions;
j) If the victim is a witness, declarant, expert, injured party or offended party and the crime is committed with the purpose of preventing testimony, denunciation of the facts or the filing of a complaint or because of their intervention in the proceedings.
the agent is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.

###### Article 140 Negligent homicide

1. Whoever, through negligence, kills another person, is punished with imprisonment up to 4 years or a fine.
2. In cases where the agent has acted with gross negligence, he is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years.

###### Artigo 141º* Interrupção da gravidez

1.	Whoever, by any means and without the consent of the pregnant woman, causes her to abort is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2.	Whoever, by any means and with the consent of the pregnant woman, causes her to abort is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.
3.	The pregnant woman who gives consent to an abortion performed by a third party, or who, by her own act or that of another, causes herself to abort, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.
4.	The provisions of the preceding paragraphs do not apply when the termination of pregnancy constitutes, in accordance with the state of medical knowledge and experience, and after all possible actions have been carried out to save the life of the pregnant woman and that of the foetus or embryo, the only means to remove danger of death to the pregnant woman, provided it is performed, upon medical certification, by a doctor or other health professional under his direction in a public or

officially recognised health establishment, and with the consent of the pregnant woman.
5.	The verification of the circumstances referred to in the preceding paragraph is certified in a medical certificate written and signed by a panel of three doctors, on a date prior to that of the termination, of which the doctor who performs or supervises the performance of the termination of pregnancy is not a member.
6.	Consent is given in writing in a document signed by the pregnant woman – after hearing, if possible, the spouse or the person who lives with her in conditions analogous to those of spouses – or by another person on her behalf, whenever possible at least two days in advance of the date of the termination.
7.	In the case where the pregnant woman is a minor, consent is given by the legal representative.
8.	In the case where the pregnant woman of full age or emancipated is or becomes psychically incapable, consent is given, respectively and successively, by the spouse or the person who lives with her in conditions analogous to those of spouses, by the legal representative, by an ascendant or descendant, or, in their absence, by any relatives of the collateral line.
9.	When it is not possible to obtain the medical certificate referred to in paragraph 5, and/or it is not possible to obtain consent under the terms of paragraphs 6 to 8, and the performance of the termination is urgent and cannot be postponed, the doctor decides in conscience in view of the situation, resorting, whenever possible, to the opinion of other doctors.
10.	The panel referred to in paragraph 5 is constituted by doctors possessing adequate knowledge for the assessment of the circumstances that justify the termination of pregnancy, including, whenever possible, a gynaecologist/obstetrician.
11.	The doctors who issue the medical certificate and the doctors who perform or supervise the performance of the termination of pregnancy must provide all necessary clarifications to the pregnant woman and, if applicable, to the persons referred to in paragraphs 7 and 8, namely the method of termination to be used, the effects of the termination, the possible consequences for the physical and psychological health of the pregnant woman.

12.	Doctors and other health professionals are recognised as having the right to conscientious objection with respect to any acts relating to the termination of pregnancy.
13.	Doctors and other health professionals who invoke conscientious objection must guarantee immediate intervention in the necessary acts, as well as the immediate accompaniment of the pregnant woman, by another health professional.
14.	Conscientious objection is expressed and communicated, through a written and signed document, to the clinical head of the health establishment where the objector provides service.

* Wording introduced by law 6/2009, of 15 July The previous wording of article 141º was as follows:

###### Artigo 141º Interrupção da gravidez

1.	Whoever, by any means and without the consent of the pregnant woman, causes her to abort is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2.	Whoever, by any means and with the consent of the pregnant woman, causes her to abort is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.
3.	The pregnant woman who gives consent to an abortion performed by a third party, or who, by her own act or that of another, causes herself to abort, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.
4.	The provisions of the preceding paragraphs do not apply to cases in which the termination of pregnancy constitutes the only means to remove danger of death or of serious and irreversible injury to the body or to the physical or psychological health of the pregnant woman or of the foetus, provided it is performed, upon authorisation and supervision of a medical board, by a doctor or health professional in a public health establishment and with the consent of the pregnant woman and or of the spouse.
5.	The provisions of paragraph 4 of this article are the subject of autonomous legislation.

###### Artigo 142º Infanticídio

The mother who kills her child during childbirth or immediately after it and still under its disturbing influence is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.

###### Artigo 143º Exposição ou abandono

1.	Whoever, intentionally, places the life of another person in danger:
a)	By exposing them in a place that subjects them to a situation from which they alone cannot defend themselves; or
b)	By abandoning them without defence, by reason of age, physical disability or illness, whenever the agent has the duty to guard, watch over or assist them;
is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 6 years
2.	If from the act there results:
a)	A serious offence to physical integrity, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years;
b)	Death, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.
3.	If the victim is the spouse, descendant, ascendant, collateral, relative by affinity up to the second degree, adopter or adoptee of the agent or person who lives with him in conditions analogous to those of spouses, the penalties referred to in the preceding paragraphs are increased by one third in their limits.

###### Artigo 144º Incitamento ou auxílio ao suicídio

1.	Whoever incites another person to commit suicide, or provides them with assistance for that purpose, if the suicide actually comes to be attempted or consummated, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	If the acts described in the preceding paragraph have as addressees any of the persons referred to in paragraph 3 of the preceding article or a minor under 17 years of age or a person whose capacity for evaluation or determination is significantly diminished, the penalty is imprisonment up to 5 years.

3.	Whoever, in any form and repeatedly makes public propaganda of suicide, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

##### CAPÍTULO II

CONTRA A INTEGRIDADE FÍSICA

###### Artigo 145º

Ofensas à integridade física simples

1.	Whoever offends the body or health of another person is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 146º

Ofensas à integridade física graves

Whoever offends the body or health of another person with the purpose of:
a)	Depriving them of an important organ or limb;
b)	Disfiguring them seriously and permanently;
c)	Affecting the capacity to work, intellectual capacities, or capacity to procreate in a serious and lasting manner or permanently;
d)	Causing permanent illness or incurable psychological anomaly; or
e)	Creating danger to life;
is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Artigo 147º Agravação

1.	Whoever, intending only to offend the body or health of another person:
a)	Causes the offences provided for in article 146º is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years;
b)	Causes death by negligence, is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 6 years.
2.	Whoever, intending to cause any of the offences provided for in article 146º, by negligence comes to cause death is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.

3.	If the crimes referred to in the two preceding articles have as victims any of the persons mentioned in subparagraph i) of article 139º, by reason of or in the exercise of the functions enumerated, the penalty shall be increased by one third in its maximum and minimum limits, if more serious punishment is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.

###### Artigo 148º

Ofensas à integridade física negligentes

1.	Whoever, through negligence, injures the body or health of another person, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2.	If the negligence is gross, the offender is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
3.	If the act results in serious bodily harm, the offender is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
4.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 149º

Intervenções e tratamentos médico-cirúrgicos

1.	Interventions and other treatments which, according to the state of medical knowledge and experience, are indicated and are carried out, in accordance with the leges artis, by a doctor or other person legally authorized to undertake them with the intention of preventing, diagnosing, combating or alleviating a disease, suffering, bodily injury or fatigue or a mental disturbance are not considered bodily harm.
2.	If the violation of the leges artis results in danger to the body, health or life of the patient, the offender shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
3.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 150º

Ofensas por meio de substâncias venenosas
1.	Whoever injures the body or health of another by administering poisonous or harmful substances to their physical or mental health is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years.
2.	If the act results in any of the consequences provided for in Article 146º or the death of the victim, the offender is punished, respectively, with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years and from 4 to 12 years.

###### Artigo 151º

Ofensas corporais recíprocas

1.	When two persons mutually injure each other in body or health, neither acting in self-defense and none of the effects provided for in Article 146º nor the death of any of the participants occurring, they are punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 152º Participação em rixa

1.	Whoever intervenes or takes part in a fight with two or more persons, from which death or serious bodily harm results, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine if those effects cannot be intentionally imputed to them.
2.	Participation in a fight is not punishable when it is determined by a non-censurable motive, namely if it aims to react against an attack, defend another or separate the combatants.

###### Artigo 153º Maus-tratos a incapaz

1.	Whoever has in their custody or care, or under the responsibility of their education, or, further, as a subordinate in work activity, an incapable person, particularly vulnerable by reason of illness, advanced age, pregnancy, physical or mental disability, and causes them bodily or health injuries, or inflicts physical or psychological ill-treatment, or cruel treatment, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.
2.	If the victim is a descendant, collateral, relative or affine up to the second degree, adoptee of the offender or person who cohabits with them in analogous conditions, the penalty referred to in the preceding number is increased by one third in its limits.

###### Artigo 154º Maus-tratos a cônjuge

Whoever inflicts on their spouse or on a person with whom they cohabit in a situation analogous to that of spouses physical or psychological ill-treatment or cruel treatment is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable under another legal provision.

###### Artigo 155º Maus-tratos a menor

1.	Whoever, having in their custody or care a minor under 17 years of age, is responsible for their education or has them as a subordinate in work activity, and:
a)	Causes bodily or health injuries, inflicts physical or psychological ill-treatment, or cruel treatment
b)	Subjects them to economic exploitation, to dangerous work or work capable of compromising their education or their physical, mental, spiritual, moral or social development;
c)	Subjects them to any form of slavery or analogous practice;
d)	Uses, recruits or offers them for purposes of prostitution, production of pornographic material or pornographic shows; or
e)	Uses, recruits or offers them for the practice of illicit acts or activities, namely for the production and trafficking of narcotics as defined by international conventions;
is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.

2.	Whoever, in the same situation, uses the minor for begging, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.
3.	If the victim is a descendant, collateral, relative or affine up to the second degree, adoptee of the offender or person who cohabits with them in analogous conditions, the penalties referred to in the preceding numbers are increased by one third in their limits.

###### Artigo 156º Agravação pelo resultado

If, as a consequence of the ill-treatment described in Articles 153º to 155º, the effects referred to in Article 146º occur, the offender is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years and if death is caused the penalty is from 5 to 15 years of imprisonment.

##### CAPÍTULO III

CONTRA A LIBERDADE PESSOAL

###### SECÇÃO I AGRESSÕES PESSOAIS

###### Artigo 157º Ameaças

1.	Whoever, by any means, threatens another person with the commission of a crime in such a way as to cause them fear or unease or to prejudice their freedom of determination, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 158º Coacção

1.	Whoever, by means of violence or threat of significant harm, compels another person to an action or omission, or to endure an activity, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 159º Coacção Grave

If the coercion is carried out:
a)	By means of the threat of a crime punishable with imprisonment exceeding 3 years,
b)	By an official seriously abusing their functions,
c)	Against a particularly defenseless person, by reason of age, disability, illness or pregnancy
d)	Against any of the persons referred to in paragraph i) of Article 139º
the offender is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 160º Sequestro

1.	Whoever, outside the cases provided for in criminal procedural law, detains, imprisons, keeps imprisoned or detained another person, or in any other way deprives them of liberty, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	The penalty of imprisonment is from 2 to 8 years if the deprivation of liberty:
a)	Lasts more than seventy-two hours;
b)	Is effected by means of injury to physical integrity, torture or any other cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment;
c)	Causes, through the offender's negligence, the death of the victim, or has as a result the victim's suicide;
d)	Has as victim one who exercises public, religious or political authority.
e)	Is promoted, authorized or supported by an agent of public authority or member of a political organization.

###### Artigo 161º Rapto

1.	Whoever, by means of violence, threat or cunning, transfers from one place to another, another person with the intention of:
a)	Subjecting the victim to extortion;
b)	Committing a crime of aggression, exploitation or sexual abuse;
c)	Obtaining ransom or reward; or
d)	Compelling the public authority or a third party to perform an action or omission, or to endure an activity;
is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.
2.	The applicable penalty is from 5 to 15 years of imprisonment if any of the circumstances provided for in number 2 of Article 160º is verified.

###### Article 162 Slavery

1. Whoever, by any means, places another human being in a situation of slavery and makes use of them in that condition, is punished with imprisonment from 8 to 20 years.
2. The consent of the victim is irrelevant, if any of the means referred to in the following article have been used.
3. For the purposes of applying the provisions of this article, a person is considered to be in a situation of slavery when in a state of submission, even if de facto, to powers corresponding to those of a property right, or of any real right, or bound to the destination of a thing.

###### Article 163 Trafficking in persons

1. Whoever recruits, alienates, transfers, acquires, transports, conveys, lodges or harbours persons, by resorting to threat, use of force or other forms of coercion, abduction, fraud, deception, abuse of authority or of a situation of vulnerability, or by means of the giving or acceptance of payments or benefits, to obtain the consent of a person having authority over another, in order to

achieve the purposes of exploitation, is punished with imprisonment from 8 to 20 years.
2. The penalty provided for in the preceding paragraph is incurred by whoever recruits, transports, conveys, lodges or harbours a minor under 17 years of age for purposes of exploitation even if it does not involve any of the means referred to in the preceding paragraph.
3. For the purposes of applying the provisions of this article, exploitation must include, at least, the exploitation of the prostitution of others or other forms of sexual exploitation, forced labour or services, slavery or practices similar to slavery, servitude or the extraction of organs.
4. The consent of the victim is irrelevant, if any of the means referred to in paragraph 1 have been used.

###### Article 164 Aggravation

If the acts described in articles 162 and 163 are committed:
a) As a way of facilitating the exploitation or sexual use of the victim, by the agent or by a third party;
b) The victim being a minor under 17 years of age;
c) The victim being in a foreign country or being moved there;
d) Using the victim, against their will, in the commission of crimes; or
e) The agent performing an activity that confers upon them public or religious authority before a group, region or the entirety of the country;
the agent is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.

###### Article 165 Trafficking in human organs

1. Whoever obtains, alienates, transfers, acquires, transports or conveys tissues, organs, substances or parts of the human body of a third party, without consent or by resorting to threat, use of force or other forms of coercion, abduction, fraud, deception, abuse of authority or of a situation of vulnerability, or by means of the giving or

acceptance of payments or benefits, or assists in their obtaining, transaction, transport or storage, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.
2. If from the commission of the facts referred to in the preceding paragraph any of the consequences provided for in article 146 or the death of the victim results, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years and from 5 to 20 years, respectively.
3. The consent of the victim is criminally irrelevant, if any of the means referred to in paragraph 1 has been used.

###### Article 166 Sale of persons

1. Whoever, outside the situations provided for in article 163, by any act or other form of transaction, transfers a person or group of persons to another person or group of persons by means of payment of any amount or other consideration, reward or advantage, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years;
2. If the facts referred to in the preceding paragraph are committed:
a) In relation to a minor under 17 years of age
b) Abusing authority resulting from a family relationship, guardianship or curatorship, hierarchical, economic or work dependence;
c) Taking advantage of the functions or position that, in any capacity, they exercise in a prison establishment, educational or correctional establishment, hospital, hospice, asylum, clinic or other health establishment or other establishment intended for assistance or treatment; or
d) Upon an unconscious or incapable person, particularly vulnerable by reason of illness, physical or mental disability
the agent is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.
3. The consent of the person themselves or of a third party who exercises any form of authority over the victim is criminally irrelevant.

###### Article 167

Torture or other cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment

1. Whoever, having the function of prevention, investigation, decision, in relation to any type of offence, the execution of the respective sanctions or the protection, custody, surveillance or accompaniment of a detained or imprisoned person and tortures or treats in a cruel, degrading or inhuman manner, in order to:
a) Obtain from them or from another person a confession, testimony, statement or information
b) Punish them for an act committed or allegedly committed by them or by another,
c) Intimidate them or to intimidate another person, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2. The penalty provided for in the preceding paragraph is also incurred by whoever, on their own initiative, by order of a superior or in agreement with the competent authority to exercise the functions referred to in the preceding paragraph, assumes de facto the performance of those functions by committing any of the acts described therein.
3. Torture, cruel, degrading or inhuman treatment is considered to be the act consisting of inflicting acute physical or psychological suffering, severe physical or psychological fatigue or the use of chemical products, drugs or other means, natural or artificial, with the intention of disturbing the capacity for decision or free manifestation of will of the victim.

###### Article 168 Aggravation

1. Whoever, under the terms and conditions described in the preceding article:
a) Produces serious bodily injury, under the terms provided for in article 146;
b) Employs particularly serious means or methods of torture, namely beating, electroshock, mock

execution, hallucinogenic substances, sexual abuse or serious threat against family members;
c) Commits such acts as a way of preventing or hindering the free exercise of constitutionally enshrined political or associative rights;
d) Habitually commits the acts referred to therein; is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.
2. If from the acts referred to in this article or in the preceding article the suicide or death of the victim results, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 20 years.

###### Article 169 Failure to report

1. The hierarchical superior who, having knowledge of the commission, by a subordinate, of any of the facts described in articles 167 and 168, does not make the report within three days immediately following knowledge thereof, is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 6 years.
2. Anyone to whom, for professional reasons and officially, knowledge is given of the commission of facts described in articles 167 and 168 and does not immediately communicate to the hierarchical superior or make the respective report, is punished with the penalty fixed in the preceding paragraph extraordinarily mitigated.

###### Article 170

Freedom of assembly or demonstration

1. Whoever interferes in an assembly or demonstration in a public place or open to the public, legally authorised, preventing or attempting to prevent its holding is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2. The agent of police authority who prevents or attempts to prevent, outside the legal conditions, the exercise of the right of assembly or demonstration described in the preceding paragraph, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.

###### SECTION II SEXUAL ASSAULTS

###### Article 171 Sexual coercion

Whoever, by means of violence, serious threat, or after, for that purpose, having rendered them unconscious or placed them in the impossibility of resisting, compels another person to suffer or to commit, with them or with another, a sexual act of relevance is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Article 172 Rape

Any person who, by the means referred to in the previous article, engages in vaginal intercourse, anal intercourse or oral intercourse with another person or compels them to endure the introduction of objects into the anus or vagina is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

###### Article 173 Aggravation

If the sexual assaults referred to in articles 171 and 172 are committed:
a) Abusing authority resulting from a family relationship, guardianship or curatorship, hierarchical, economic or employment dependence;
b) Taking advantage of the functions or position that, in any capacity, is exercised in a prison establishment, educational or correctional establishment, hospital, hospice, asylum, clinic or other health establishment or other establishment intended for assistance or treatment;
c) Against an unconscious or incapable person, particularly vulnerable by reason of illness, physical or mental disability;
d) Having as victims minors under 17 years of age;
the offender is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years, in the case of article 171, and with imprisonment from 5 to 20 years, in the case of article 172.

###### SECTION III

SEXUAL EXPLOITATION

###### Article 174 Sexual exploitation of third parties

1. Any person who, with intent to profit or making it a way of life, promotes, facilitates or in any way contributes to another person engaging in prostitution or performing other sexual acts is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.
2. The offender is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years, if any of the following circumstances occur:
a) Exploitation of a situation of abandonment or economic necessity of the victim;
b) Exercise of violence, serious threat or coercion against the victim;
c) Displacement of the victim to a country different from that in which they were born or in which they resided;
d) Retention of any identification document of the victim.

###### Article 175 Child prostitution

1. Any person who, even with the consent of the victim, commits acts of sexual exploitation referred to in the previous article in relation to a minor under 17 years of age, is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years in the case of number 1 and with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years in cases where any of the circumstances set out in number 2 of the previous article occur.
2. Any person who offers, obtains, seeks or delivers a minor under 17 years of age for purposes of child prostitution is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.

###### Article 176 Child pornography

1. Any person who uses, for predominantly sexual purposes, exposes or represents a minor under 17 years of age in the performance of any sexual activity, real or

simulated, or by any other means exhibits that sexual activity or the sexual organs of the minor, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.
2. The same penalty applies to any person who produces, distributes, disseminates, imports, exports, offers, sells or possesses any means of communication, instrument, document or record for the purposes referred to in the previous number or with the purpose of divulging such acts.

###### SECTION IV SEXUAL ABUSE

###### Article 177 Sexual abuse of a minor

1. Any person who engages in vaginal intercourse, anal intercourse or oral intercourse with a minor under 14 years of age is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 20 years.
2. Any person who performs any relevant sexual act with a minor under 14 years of age is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

###### Article 178

Sexual acts with adolescents

Any person who, being an adult and outside the situations provided for in this section, performs any relevant sexual act with a minor between 14 and 16 years of age, abusing their inexperience, is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years.

###### Article 179

Sexual abuse of a person incapable of resistance

Any person who performs a relevant sexual act with an unconscious or incapable person, particularly vulnerable by reason of illness, physical or mental disability, taking advantage of their state of incapacity is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.

###### Article 180 Sexual fraud

1. Any person who, fraudulently taking advantage of an error concerning their personal identity, has vaginal intercourse, anal intercourse or oral intercourse or a relevant sexual act with another person is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.
2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 181 Sexual exhibitionism

1. Any person who publicly annoys another person with the performance of acts of a sexual nature is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2. The same penalty is incurred by those who, in front of another person, engage in vaginal intercourse, anal intercourse or oral intercourse, against the will of that person and even if in private.
3. Attempt is punishable.
4. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### SECTION V COMMON PROVISIONS

###### Article 182 Aggravation

1. The penalties provided for in section II to section IV of this chapter are aggravated by one third in their minimum and maximum limits, if:
a) The victim was less than 12 years of age at the time of commission of the acts;
b) The offender transmitted to the victim a venereal disease, syphilitic disease or acquired immunodeficiency syndrome;
c) As a consequence of the acts the victim attempts or consummates suicide or death results;
d) The victim is a descendant, collateral, family member or relative by affinity up to the second degree, adoptee of the offender or person who lives with them in analogous conditions or there exists a hierarchical, economic or employment dependence.
2. In the case where several of the circumstances set out in the previous number concur, only one is relevant as

modifying the abstract framework of the legal type, with the remaining being valued in the determination of the concrete penalty.

##### CHAPTER V AGAINST PRIVATE LIFE

###### Article 183 Invasion of privacy

1. Any person who by any means, even lawful, obtains knowledge of facts relating to the intimacy of the private or sexual life of another person and, without consent, publicly discloses them without just cause, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 184 Violation of secrecy

1. Any person who, without consent, reveals another's secret of which they have obtained knowledge by reason of their status, office, employment, profession or art, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2. If the secret relates to commercial, industrial, professional or artistic activity, of which the offender has obtained knowledge in the circumstances described previously, and causes harm to another person or to the State, the penalty is 2 years of imprisonment or a fine.
3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 185 Violation of domicile

1. Any person who, without consent, enters the dwelling of another person or, authorized to enter, remains there after being ordered to leave, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2. If the offender, to more easily commit the crime, takes advantage of the night, the fact that the dwelling is located in a remote place, being 3 or more persons committing the act, uses a weapon, uses violence or threat of violence or

acts by means of climbing, breaking in or false key, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
3. If there are persons inside the dwelling when the offender commits the crime, the penalty provided for in the previous number aggravated by one third in its limits is applicable.
4. Attempt is punishable.
5. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 186

Entry into other places closed to the public

1. Any person who commits the acts described in numbers 1 and 2 of the previous article in relation to any other closed or restricted place not freely accessible to the public, is punished, respectively, with the penalties referred to in those numbers reduced by half in the maximum limits.
2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 187º

Violation of correspondence or telecommunications

1.	Whoever, without consent or outside procedurally admissible cases, opens a package, letter or any other writing intended for another person, or takes knowledge of its content, or prevents it from being received by its addressee, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2.	The same penalty is incurred by whoever, in the same circumstances, interferes with or takes knowledge of the content of telephone, telegraph or any other means of telecommunication communication.
3.	Whoever discloses the content of letters, packages, sealed writings, telephone calls or other communications referred to in the preceding numbers, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine, even if they had knowledge of that content lawfully.
4.	If the acts referred to in the preceding numbers are committed by an employee of postal, telegraph, telephone or telecommunications services, the penalties are increased by one third in their limits.

5.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

#### TÍTULO III

CRIMES AGAINST LIFE IN DEMOCRACY

##### CAPÍTULO I

AGAINST PUBLIC TRANQUILLITY

###### Artigo 188º Criminal association

1.	Whoever promotes or founds a group, organisation or association whose purpose or activity is directed to the commission of crimes, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2.	A criminal group, organisation or association is considered to be any grouping of two or more persons who, over a period of time and acting in concert, aim to commit or incite the commission of crimes, with the intention of disturbing public order or obtaining directly or indirectly a benefit or advantage.
3.	Whoever joins, supports or participates in any of the activities of criminal groups, organisations or associations, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.
4.	Whoever leads or directs the groups, organisations or associations referred to in the preceding numbers is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.
5.	The penalties referred to may be extraordinarily mitigated if the agent prevents or seriously endeavours to prevent the continuation of the groups, organisations or associations, or communicates their existence to the authority so that it can prevent the commission of crimes.

###### Artigo 189º Instigation to commit a crime

1.	Whoever, publicly and by any means, incites the commission of a crime is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

2.	Whoever, privately or publicly, praises or rewards someone who has committed a crime in such a way as, by such conduct, to incite the commission of identical crimes is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
3.	If in the case of the preceding numbers the crime whose commission the agent had instigated comes to be committed, the penalty of imprisonment is from 2 to 5 years, if a more serious one does not correspond to it by virtue of another legal provision.

###### Artigo 190º Participation in riot

1.	Whoever takes part in a public riot, during which violence against persons or property is committed collectively, is punished with imprisonment of 1 year or a fine, if no more serious penalty is applicable to them for participation in the crime committed.
2.	If the agent provoked or directed the riot, they are punished with imprisonment up to 3 years.
3.	If the riot is armed, the penalties referred to in the preceding numbers are doubled in their limits.

###### Artigo 191º

Impediment of the exercise of political rights

Whoever prevents another, through violence or threat, from exercising their political rights, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### Artigo 192º Influence peddling

1.	Whoever, by themselves or through an intermediary, with their consent or ratification, solicits or accepts, for themselves or for a third party, a patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage, or its promise, to abuse their influence, real or supposed, with any public entity, is punished:
a)	With imprisonment from 2 to 6 years, if a more serious penalty is not applicable to them by virtue of another legal provision, if the purpose is to obtain any favourable unlawful decision;

b)	With imprisonment up to 1 year or with a fine, if a more serious penalty is not applicable to them by virtue of another legal provision, if the purpose is to obtain any favourable lawful decision.
2.	Whoever, by themselves or through an intermediary, with their consent or ratification, gives or promises a patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage to the persons referred to in the preceding number for the purposes provided for in paragraph a) of the preceding number is punished with imprisonment up to 4 years or with a fine.

###### Artigo 193º Disobedience to order of dispersal

1.	Whoever does not obey a lawful order to withdraw from a gathering or public meeting, given by a competent authority, with warning that disobedience constitutes a crime, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2.	If the disobedient person is a promoter of the meeting or gathering, they are punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 194º

Abuse of public signals or uniform

1.	Whoever abusively uses an alarm or distress signal or call, or falsely causes it to be believed that assistance from others is necessary by virtue of disaster, danger or situation of collective necessity, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or with a fine.
2.	The same penalty is incurred by whoever improperly or abusively uses uniforms, dress, attire or insignia identifying activities, authorities or public or international institutions as a means of more easily committing any unlawful act.

###### Artigo 195º Usurpation of functions

1.	Whoever, without being authorised to do so, exercises functions or performs acts proper to an official, military command or public security force, arrogating to themselves, expressly or tacitly, that quality, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	The same penalty is incurred by whoever exercises a profession for which the law requires a title or fulfilment of certain conditions, arrogating to themselves, expressly or tacitly, possessing it or fulfilling them, when, in fact, they do not possess it or do not fulfil them.
3.	The same penalty is incurred by whoever continues in the exercise of public functions, after having been officially notified of dismissal or suspension of those functions.

##### CAPÍTULO II

AGAINST THE SECURITY OF THE STATE

###### Artigo 196º Treason against the Homeland

Whoever, by means of violence, threat of violence, usurpation or abuse of functions of sovereignty, prevents or attempts to prevent the exercise of national sovereignty in the territory or in part of the territory of Timor-Leste or endangers the integrity of the national territory, as a form of submission or surrender to foreign sovereignty, is punished with imprisonment from 15 to 30 years.

###### Artigo 197º

Service or collaboration with enemy armed forces

1.	The Timorese citizen who collaborates with a foreign country or groups or with their representatives, or who serves under the flag of a foreign country during war or armed action against Timor-Leste, is punished with imprisonment from 12 to 25 years.
2.	Preparatory acts relating to the facts described in the preceding number are punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years
3.	Whoever, being Timorese or resident in the national territory, commits acts adequate to help or facilitate

any armed action or war against Timor-Leste by a foreign country or group, is punished with imprisonment from 10 to 20 years.

###### Artigo 198º

Sabotage against national defence

Whoever, with the intention of harming or endangering national defence, destroys, damages or renders unusable, totally or partially:
a)	Works or materials proper to or assigned to the armed forces;
b)	Routes or means of communication or transport;
c)	Any other installations related to communications or transport; or
d)	Factories or depots;
is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 199º

Campanha contra esforço pela paz

Any person who, being Timorese or resident in national territory, in time of preparation for or of war, disseminates by any means, so as to make public, rumours or statements, their own or those of others, which they know to be, wholly or partially, false, in order to prejudice the peace effort of Timor-Leste or to assist the foreign enemy, is punished with a prison sentence of 5 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 200º

Violação de segredo de Estado

1.	Whoever, endangering the interest of the Timorese State relating to its external security or the conduct of its foreign policy, transmits, makes accessible to an unauthorised person or makes public any fact, document, plan, object, knowledge or any other information that should, by reason of that interest, remain secret in relation to a foreign country, is punished with a prison sentence of 3 to 10 years.

2.	Whoever collaborates with a foreign government or group with the intention of committing the acts referred to in the preceding number or recruits or assists another person charged with committing them, is punished with the penalty provided for in the preceding number.
3.	If the agent who commits the acts described in the preceding numbers exercises any political, public or military function which, by its nature, should inhibit them from committing such acts more strongly than the ordinary citizen, they are punished with a prison sentence of 5 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 201º Infidelidade diplomática

Any person who, officially representing the Timorese State, with the intention of prejudicing national rights or interests:
a)	Conducts State business with a foreign government or international organisation; or
b)	Assumes commitments in the name of Timor-Leste without being duly authorised to do so;
is punished with a prison sentence of 5 to 15 years.

###### Artigo 202º Alteração do Estado de Direito

1.	Whoever, by means of violence, threat of violence or incitement to civil war, attempts to destroy, alter or subjugate the constitutionally established rule of law, is punished with a sentence of 5 to 15 years' imprisonment.
2.	If the act described above is committed by means of armed violence, the sentence is 5 to 20 years' imprisonment.
3.	Public incitement or the distribution of weapons for the commission of the acts described above is, respectively, punished with the penalty corresponding to an attempt.

###### Artigo 203º

Atentado contra representante máximo de órgão de soberania

1.	Whoever attempts against the life, physical integrity or liberty of the highest representative of an organ of sovereignty or of whoever constitutionally replaces them or of whoever has been elected or appointed to the office, even before taking office, is punished with a prison sentence of 8 to 20 years.
2.	In the case of consummation of a crime against life, physical integrity or liberty, the agent is punished with a prison sentence of 12 to 25 years.
3.	The penalties provided for in the preceding numbers are correspondingly applicable whenever the acts described therein are committed against a foreign person who is in the situation referred to in number 1, ambassadors and holders of leading organs of international organisations, when they are in Timor-Leste.

###### Artigo 204º

Coacção contra órgãos constitucionais

1.	Whoever, by violence or threat of violence, prevents or constrains the free exercise of the functions of an organ of sovereignty is punished with a prison sentence of 3 to 10 years.
2.	If the acts described in the preceding number are committed against organs of district or local power, the sentence is 2 to 6 years' imprisonment.
3.	If the acts referred to described in number 1 are committed against members of those organs, the penalties referred to in numbers 1 and 2 are reduced to half their limits.

###### Artigo 205º

Perturbação de funcionamento de órgão constitucional

Any person who, by means of tumults, disorders or shouting, unlawfully disturbs the functioning of the organs referred to in the preceding article or the exercise of functions by any of the persons who are part of them, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### Artigo 206º

Ultraje de símbolos nacionais

Whoever, publicly, by words, by gestures or dissemination of writings, or by any other means of communication with the public, outrages the flag or the national anthem, the arms or emblems of Timorese sovereignty or fails to show the respect due to them, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

##### CAPÍTULO III

CONTRA A VIDA EM SOCIEDADE

###### SECÇÃO I

CRIMES DE PERIGO COMUM

###### Artigo 207º Condução sem carta

Whoever uses a motorised vehicle without being qualified with the legally required licence, is punished with a prison sentence up to 2 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 208º

Condução sob o efeito do álcool ou de substâncias psicotrópicas

1.	Whoever, at least through negligence, drives a motorised vehicle with more than 1.2mg of alcohol per litre of blood, is punished with a prison sentence up to 2 years or a fine.
2.	The same penalty is incurred by whoever, at least through negligence, drives a motorised vehicle, not being in a condition to do so safely, by being under the influence of a narcotic, psychotropic substance or product with an analogous disturbing effect on physical, mental or psychological aptitude.

###### Artigo 209º Condução perigosa

1.	Whoever drives any motorised vehicle on a public road and, by not being in a condition to do so safely or by grossly violating the rules of

road traffic, creates danger to the life or physical integrity of another person is punished with a prison sentence of 1 to 4 years.
2.	Negligence in relation to the conduct or the danger is punished with a prison sentence up to 2 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 210º

Atentado contra a segurança de transportes

1.	Whoever commits any act adequate to cause the lack or diminution of safety in a means of transport and, in this way, creates a danger to the life or physical integrity of another person, is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 8 years.
2.	The provisions of number 2 of the preceding article are correspondingly applicable.

###### Artigo 211º Armas proibidas

1.	Any person who, outside legal prescriptions, manufactures, imports, transports, sells or transfers to another person firearms, chemical weapons, biological weapons, nuclear weapons, ammunition for them, substances for their manufacture or operation or any other type of explosive, is punished with a prison sentence of 2 to 6 years.
2.	If the acts described in the preceding number have as their purpose use for military purposes, the sentence is 2 to 8 years' imprisonment.
3.	The mere possession, use or carrying of a firearm without the agent being legally authorised, is punishable with a prison sentence up to 2 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 212º Embriaguez e intoxicação

1.	Whoever, at least through negligence, places themselves in a state of non-imputability derived from the ingestion or consumption of alcoholic beverage or toxic substance and in that state commits a typical unlawful act is punished with a prison sentence up to 5 years or a fine.

2.	The penalty cannot exceed that provided for the typical unlawful act committed.
3.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 213º

Habilitação para o exercício de certas actividades

1.	Whoever, without being legally qualified, sells, administers, prescribes or transfers in any form, habitually, to other persons, pharmaceutical products or others whose trade and prescription, administration or transfer, are reserved to health professionals or other duly licensed entities, is punished with a prison sentence up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	If as a consequence of the preceding acts danger to the life of another person results, the sentence is 1 to 4 years' imprisonment.

###### Artigo 214º

Produtos adulterados ou deteriorados
