1.	Whoever places for sale, administers or transfers in any form to another person food products, pharmaceuticals, or others which, being deteriorated, adulterated or contaminated, are likely to endanger life is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2.	If from the commission of the acts described in the preceding number results the death of the victim due to the consumption of such products, the penalty is from 3 to 12 years.

###### SECÇÃO II

CRIMES CONTRA O AMBIENTE

###### Artigo 215º Contra o ambiente

1.	Whoever, not observing legal or regulatory provisions protecting the environment, causes or carries out directly or indirectly emissions, discharges, radiations, extractions or excavations, ignitions, noises, vibrations, injections or deposits, in the atmosphere, in the soil,

in the subsoil or in terrestrial, maritime or underground waters, including in cross-border zones, or water abstraction that may seriously harm the balance of natural systems, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	If the agent intentionally releases, emits or introduces ionising radiation or other substances into the air, land or maritime, continental, surface or underground waters, in a quantity that produces in one or more persons serious bodily harm requiring medical or surgical treatment or produces irreversible sequelae, he is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years and if death is caused the penalty is from 5 to 15 years of imprisonment.

###### Artigo 216º Agravação

1.	If the acts or activities provided for in the preceding article are committed by an industrial or commercial establishment and any of the following circumstances occurs:
a)	The industry or commercial activity operates clandestinely without the proper licence or administrative authorisation
b)	When express orders of the competent administrative authority for the correction or suspension of the activities referred to in the preceding article have been disobeyed
c)	When the safety rules or procedures provided for in legal or regulatory provisions have not been complied with.
d)	When, by any means, the inspection process of the competent administrative authority has been intentionally impeded or information about the environmental consequences of the industry or commercial activity has been omitted or falsified.
e)	When an irreversible or catastrophic state of environmental deterioration has been produced
the penalties provided for in the preceding number are aggravated by one third in their minimum and maximum limits.
2.	Criminally responsible, under the terms of the preceding number, are the individual holder, the legal representatives, or

those who act on behalf of the legal person holding the infringing industrial or commercial establishments, as well as the partners or members who authorise them to act, when it is irregularly constituted.

###### Artigo 217º Contra a flora ou fauna

1.	Whoever with serious harm to the environment cuts, burns, uproots, collects or carries out illegal trafficking of any species of flora or its seeds, classified as threatened or at risk of extinction, destroying or seriously altering its natural habitat, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	Incurs the penalty provided for in the preceding number, whoever introduces or facilitates the entry of non-native species of flora or fauna, in a way that harms the biological balance, violating the laws or provisions of a general nature, protecting species of flora or fauna.

###### Artigo 218º

Contra espécies ameaçadas ou em risco de extinção

1.	Whoever hunts or fishes threatened species or carries out any activity that prevents their development or hinders their reproduction or migration, violating laws or provisions of a general nature protecting species of wild fauna, as well as commercialises or traffics them, in whole or in part, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	If the acts referred to in the preceding number are committed:
a)	In terrestrial or maritime zones declared protected natural zones;
b)	Against species or subspecies classified as endangered;
the agent is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 219º Pesca ilegal

1.	Whoever fishes in national maritime waters without the proper fishing licence obtained from the competent administrative entity, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	If the agent is a legal person, criminally responsible, under the terms of the preceding number, are the legal representatives, or those who act on behalf of the legal person, as well as the partners or members who authorise them to act, when it is irregularly constituted.
3.	Not punishable under the terms of number one, is fishing practised for domestic subsistence.

###### Artigo 220º Meios de pesca ilícitos

Whoever uses firearms, explosives, toxic substances or other instruments or similar arts of destructive efficacy for maritime fauna, for the capture of fish resources, in national terrestrial or maritime waters, is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 221º Queimada proibida

1.	Whoever carries out burning outside the proper season or without administrative authorisation, when necessary, resulting in the destruction of forest, plantation or crop is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2.	If the burning is legally carried out but, through negligence, the agent causes the damage referred to in the preceding number, the penalty is imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### SECÇÃO III OUTROS CRIMES

###### Artigo 222º

Impedimento ou perturbação de cortejo, cerimónia ou culto

1.	Whoever prevents or disturbs the holding of a funeral procession or ceremony or the exercise of religious worship by means of violence or threat of violence or any other form of coercion, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 223º

Profanação de objecto ou lugar de culto ou veneração

1.	Whoever, in a manner to cause alarm or disturb social peace, desecrates a place or object of worship or religious veneration, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2.	Incurs the same penalty whoever offends or insults another person because of their belief or religious function, in a manner adequate to cause alarm or social disturbance.
3.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 224º

Destruição, subtracção, ocultação ou profanação de cadáver

1.	Whoever, against or without the will of the rightful person and outside the cases in which the law permits it, removes, destroys or conceals corpses or parts thereof, or ashes of a deceased person, shall be punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2.	Incurs the same penalty whoever desecrates corpses, parts of corpses or ashes of deceased persons, committing acts offensive to the respect due to the dead.
3.	Attempt is punishable.
4.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 225º

Não cumprimento de obrigação alimentar

1.	Whoever is obliged to provide maintenance, has the means to do so and fails to fulfil the obligation in a manner to endanger the satisfaction of the fundamental needs of the person entitled to maintenance, is punished with a penalty of

imprisonment up to 3 years or with a fine, even if assistance provided by another removes the danger.
2.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 226º Subtracção de menor

1.	Whoever removes or refuses to hand over a minor to the person to whom their custody has been entrusted or causes the minor to flee, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	If the acts are committed with violence or threat of violence, the penalty is imprisonment from 1 to 4 years.
3.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 227º Omissão de auxílio

1.	Whoever, in a case of grave necessity, namely caused by disaster, accident, public calamity or situation of common danger, that endangers the life, physical integrity or freedom of another person, fails to provide the necessary assistance to remove the danger, whether by personal action or by promoting rescue, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2.	If the situation referred to in the preceding number was created by the person who omits the due assistance, the omitter is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
3.	The omission of assistance is not punishable when there is grave risk to the life or physical integrity of the omitter or when, for another relevant reason, assistance is not required of them.
4.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 228º Recusa de auxílio médico

A doctor or health professional who refuses the assistance of their profession in a case of danger to life or grave danger to the physical integrity of another person, which cannot be removed in another way, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

##### CAPÍTULO IV CRIMES ELEITORAIS

###### Artigo 229º Fraude no recenseamento

1.	Whoever prevents another person, whom they know has the right, from registering, causes facts which they know are not true to be recorded, omits facts which they should record or by any other means falsifies the electoral register, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	If the person is prevented from registering or convinced to register by means of violence or cunningly provoked deception, the applicable penalty is imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.
3.	Attempt is punishable.

###### Artigo 230º Obstrução a candidatura

Whoever, by any means, prevents another person, party or political force whom they know has the right, from standing in an electoral act, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Artigo 231º Candidato inelegível

1.	Whoever, knowing that they do not have electoral capacity to be elected, presents their candidacy, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2.	Attempt is punishable.

###### Artigo 232º

Falta de cadernos eleitorais

Whoever, being charged with the preparation or correction of electoral registers, does not proceed with their execution or prevents the legal substitute from doing so in order to prevent the holding of an electoral act, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 233º Propaganda eleitoral ilícita

1.	Whoever uses a legally prohibited means of propaganda or continues to carry out propaganda beyond the established deadline or in a prohibited place, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2.	Whoever prevents the exercise of the right to electoral propaganda or proceeds with its unlawful destruction, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 234º Obstrução à liberdade de escolha

1.	Whoever, by means of violence, threat of violence or by fraudulent artifice, compels another person not to vote or to vote in a certain way or buys or sells votes, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	The same penalty applies to whoever, requested to assist in voting a blind person or someone legally entitled to such assistance, disregards the voting intention communicated to them.
3.	Attempt is punishable.

###### Artigo 235º Perturbação do acto eleitoral

1.	Whoever, by any means, disturbs the functioning of the polling station, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2.	The agent shall be punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years if the disturbance results from:
a)	Violence or threat of violence;
b)	Tumult or gathering of population near the polling station;
c)	Intentional cutting of electrical power;
d)	Absence of someone indispensable to the act if, for that reason, the holding of the act must be considered gravely affected in order to begin or continue.

3.	The provisions of the preceding numbers apply correspondingly if the facts are committed during the counting of results after the voting has taken place.

###### Artigo 236º

Obstrução à fiscalização do acto eleitoral

1.	Whoever, by any means, prevents the representative of any party or political force, legally constituted and standing in the electoral act, from exercising their supervisory powers, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	Attempt is punishable.

###### Artigo 237º Fraude na votação

1.	Whoever votes without having the right to vote or does so more than once in relation to the same electoral act is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	The same penalty is incurred by whoever intentionally permits the commission of the act described in the preceding number.
3.	Attempt is punishable.

###### Artigo 238º Fraude no escrutínio

1.	Whoever, by any means, vitiates the counting of votes in the act of counting or publication of electoral results, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.
2.	The same penalty is incurred by whoever, with fraudulent intent, substitutes, destroys, suppresses, violates, vitiates or falsifies voting or counting bulletins, or documents relating to the election.

###### Artigo 239º Recusa de cargo eleitoral

Whoever is appointed to be part of the polling station tables and, without justification, refuses to assume

or abandons those functions, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### Artigo 240º

Violação do segredo de escrutínio

Whoever in an electoral act conducted by secret ballot, violates such secrecy, by taking or giving knowledge of the voting intention of another person, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### Artigo 241º

Dever de neutralidade e imparcialidade

Officials of the electoral administration or who collaborate with it who violate the duties of neutrality and impartiality are punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 242º Agravação

If whoever commits any of the crimes provided for in this chapter performs public functions, namely in the Government, in the National Parliament, in the Armed Forces, as a judicial magistrate or of the Public Prosecution Service, in the various police forces or in other administrative bodies, the sanctions provided for in the type are raised to double their limits.

##### CAPÍTULO V

CONTRA A AUTORIDADE PÚBLICA

###### Artigo 243º Obstrução à autoridade pública

1.	Whoever, by means of violence or grave threat against an official or agent of military, militarized or police forces, opposes the performance of an act relating to the exercise of their functions or compels the performance of an act contrary to their duties is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

2.	If the act referred to in the preceding number is actually performed or prevented from being performed the penalty is from 2 to 8 years.

###### Artigo 244º Desobediência

1.	Whoever fails or persists in failing to obey a legitimate order or mandate, regularly communicated and coming from a competent authority or official, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine, if:
a)	A legal provision so determines; or
b)	If warned that their conduct is liable to generate criminal liability and the authority or official makes the corresponding threat.
2.	In cases where the legal provision qualifies the act as qualified disobedience the penalty is from 1 to 4 years of imprisonment.
3.	Disobedience to specific prohibitions or interdictions determined in a criminal sentence, as well as disobedience to accessory penalties or non-custodial security measures, is punishable with the penalty referred to in number 1.

###### Artigo 245º Tirada de presos

1.	Whoever, by illegal means, frees or, by any means, assists the escape of a person legally deprived of liberty, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.
2.	If the facts are committed with the use of violence, using weapons or with the collaboration of more than two persons, the penalty is imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Article 246 Escape

1. Whoever, being legally deprived of liberty, escapes, is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 4 years.

2. If the escape is achieved by any of the means described in number 2 of the previous article, the penalty is from 2 to 6 years.

###### Article 247

Official's assistance in escape

1. The official who assists in the commission of any of the acts provided for in the previous articles is punished with the penalties therein prescribed, aggravated by one third in their limits.
2. If the official was supposed to exercise custody or surveillance over the escapee and, even so, assisted in those acts, the prescribed penalties are aggravated by half in their limits.
3. If the escape is due only to gross negligence of the official in charge of surveillance of the escapee, the penalty is imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

###### Article 248 Mutiny of prisoners

1. Those who, being legally deprived of liberty, in concert and in joint effort with another person in the same circumstances, attack or threaten with violence whoever is in charge of their surveillance or custody, in order to achieve their escape or that of a third party, or to commit an act or to refrain from its commission, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2. If through the commission of the acts described in the previous number the intentions of one's own or another's escape are achieved, the penalty is from 3 to 10 years.

###### Article 249

Misappropriation or destruction of objects under public authority

Whoever destroys, damages or renders unusable, totally or partially, or in any way, removes from the public authority to which it is assigned or subject, a document or other movable object, as well as a thing that has been attached,

seized or subject to a precautionary measure, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another provision.

###### Article 250

Breaking of marks, seals and notices

1. Whoever opens, breaks or renders unusable, totally or partially, marks or seals legitimately affixed by a competent official, to identify or keep inviolable any thing, or to certify that it has been subject to attachment, seizure or precautionary measure, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2. Whoever in any way intentionally prevents the addressee from becoming aware of a notice posted by a competent official is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

#### TITLE IV

CRIMES AGAINST PROPERTY

##### CHAPTER I AGAINST OWNERSHIP

###### Article 251 Simple theft

1. Whoever, with unlawful intention of appropriation for himself or for another, removes movable property belonging to another, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2. The attempt is punishable.
3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 252 Aggravated theft

1. The agent who commits the acts referred to in the previous article is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years, if:

a) The movable property belonging to another possesses high scientific, artistic or historical value, or is important for economic or technological development;
b) The movable property belonging to another is a vehicle, transported in a vehicle or by a passenger of public transport, or is located at the dock or station for embarkation and disembarkation;
c) The movable property belonging to another is assigned to religious worship or to the veneration of the memory of the dead and is located in a place intended for worship or in a cemetery;
d) The agent takes advantage of the night to more easily commit the theft;
e) The victim suffers considerable losses as a consequence of the appropriation;
f) The agent enters a dwelling, public service facilities, commercial or industrial establishment with the intention of stealing;
g) The agent uses false keys, climbing or breaking in carrying out his design;
h) The agent takes advantage of the situation of special vulnerability of the victim of disaster, accident or public calamity;
i) The agent takes advantage of the existence of a special relationship of trust with the victim or with the owner of the place where the removed thing is located;
j) The agent makes the commission of thefts a way of life;
k) The crime is committed by 3 or more persons, including the agent;
l) The value of the removed thing is greater than 1,000 US dollars but less than or equal to 5,000 US dollars.
2. If the value of the removed thing is greater than 5,000 US dollars, the imprisonment penalty shall be from 3 to 10 years.
3. If in the same conduct more than one of the circumstances referred to in number one concur, only the one that has the strongest aggravating effect is considered for the purpose of determining the abstract framework of the applicable legal type, with the remaining ones being valued as

general circumstances in determining the measure of the penalty.
4. If the value of the stolen thing is less than 50 US dollars, the circumstances referred to in no. 1 only function as general aggravating circumstances.

###### Article 253 Robbery

1. Whoever, with unlawful intention of appropriation for himself or for another person, removes or compels the delivery to him of movable property belonging to another by means of violence against a person, threat with imminent danger to life or physical integrity or by placing the person in the impossibility of resisting, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.
2. If, simultaneously with the agent's conduct, any of the circumstances described in number 1 of the previous article occurs, the imprisonment penalty shall be from 4 to 12 years.
3. If from the agent's conduct danger to the victim's life results or serious offenses to physical integrity are caused, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 15 years.
4. If from the agent's conduct death results, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 5 to 20 years.

###### Article 254 Violence after removal

Whoever, caught in the act of theft, acts in the ways described in the previous article with the purpose of retaining or preventing the restitution of the appropriated things, is punished with the penalties for the crime of robbery respectively.

###### Article 255 Theft of use of vehicle

1. Whoever uses an automobile or other motorized vehicle, aircraft, boat or bicycle without authorization from the rightful person, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.

2. The attempt is punishable.
3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 256

Simple breach of trust

1. Whoever unlawfully appropriates movable property that has been delivered to him by a title not transferring ownership is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2. The attempt is punishable.
3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 257

Aggravated breach of trust

1. If the movable property has a value greater than 1,000 US dollars, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2. The penalties provided for in the previous number and in article 256 are increased by one third in their minimum and maximum limits if the agent received the thing in deposit imposed by law, by reason of office, employment or profession, or in the capacity of guardian, curator or depositary.

###### Article 258 Simple damage

1. Whoever, totally or partially, destroys, damages, disfigures or renders unusable property belonging to another is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2. The attempt is punishable.
3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 259 Aggravated damage

Whoever, totally or partially, destroys, damages, disfigures or renders unusable a thing:
a) Intended for public use or utility;

b) With an important scientific, artistic or historical value or possessing great importance for technological or scientific development;
c) Corresponding to a means of communication or transport of great social importance;
d) Causing a loss greater than 1,000 US dollars;
e) Belonging to another, assigned to religious worship or to the veneration of the memory of the dead and located in a place intended for worship or in a cemetery;
is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years or a fine.

###### Article 260 Damage with violence

If the acts described in articles 258 and 259 are committed with violence against a person, or threat with imminent danger to life or physical integrity, or by rendering the person unable to resist, the conduct is punished with imprisonment from 4 to 12 years.

###### Article 261 Usurpation of immovable property

1. Whoever, by means of violence or serious threat against another person, invades or occupies another's immovable property, with the intention of exercising a right of ownership, possession, use or easement not protected by law, judgment, contract or administrative act, is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 4 years.
2. If the means employed constitutes a crime punishable with a penalty greater than that provided for in the preceding paragraph, that shall be the applicable penalty.

###### Article 262 Alteration of boundary markers

1. Whoever, with the intention of appropriation, total or partial, of another's immovable property, for himself or for another person, removes or alters a boundary marker or any other sign intended to establish the limits of immovable property, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 263 Arson

1. Whoever, intending to cause a fire in a house, building, establishment, means of transport, forest, plantation or any other property and, in this manner, create danger to life, physical integrity or to patrimonial assets with a value exceeding 5,000 US dollars, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2. If the acts described in the preceding paragraph relate to public property or property in which public services operate, the penalty is 2 to 10 years of imprisonment.
3. The offender is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine if the acts are committed negligently, unless a more severe penalty applies by virtue of another legal provision.
4. If only the danger referred to in paragraph 1 is created by negligence, the penalty is 2 to 6 years of imprisonment.
5. In the cases provided for in paragraph 4, criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 264 Active repentance

If, after the commission of the crimes provided for in articles 251, 252, 256 to 261 and 263, paragraph 4, but before the commencement of the trial hearing, the offender performs acts aimed at the restitution or full or partial reparation of the damage caused, the penalty may be extraordinarily mitigated.

###### Article 265 Definitions

For the purposes of the provisions of this Code, the following are considered:
a) "Breaking", the rupture, fracture or destruction, in whole or in part, of a device intended to close or prevent entry, externally or internally, to a house or enclosed place dependent on it;

b) "Scaling", the introduction into a house or enclosed place dependent on it, through a place not normally intended for entry or through any device intended to close or prevent entry or passage;
c) "False keys",
i) Those imitated, counterfeited or altered;
ii) True keys when, fortuitously or surreptitiously, they are outside the power of the person who has the right to use them; and
iii) Lockpicks or any instruments that may be used to open locks or other security devices.

##### CHAPTER II

AGAINST PROPERTY IN GENERAL

###### Article 266 Simple fraud

1. Whoever, with the intention of obtaining for himself or for a third party unlawful enrichment, by means of error or deception about facts that he cunningly provoked, causes another person to perform acts that cause him, or cause another person, patrimonial loss, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2. Attempt is punishable.
3. The provisions of article 264 are correspondingly applicable.
4. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 267 Aggravated fraud

1. Whoever, as a result of the conduct described in the preceding article:
a) Causes loss exceeding 2,000 US dollars;
b) Makes fraud a way of life;
c) Places the injured person in a difficult economic situation; is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.

2. The provisions of article 264 are correspondingly applicable.

###### Article 268 Computer fraud

1. Whoever, with the intention of obtaining for himself or for a third party unlawful enrichment, causes another person patrimonial loss, by interfering with the result of computer data processing or by means of incorrect structuring of a computer program, incorrect or incomplete use of data, unauthorized use of data or intervention in any other unauthorized manner in the processing, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2. Attempt is punishable.
3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 269

Aggravated computer fraud

When as a result of the conduct described in the preceding article any of the circumstances described in paragraph 1 of article 267 occurs, the offender is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.

###### Article 270 Extortion

1. Whoever, with the intention of obtaining for himself or for a third party unlawful enrichment, compels another person, by means of violence or threat of significant harm, to a patrimonial disposition that entails, for him or for another, loss, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.
2. If the circumstances provided for in article 252 occur, the conduct of the offender is punished with the penalties provided therein.
3. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 271 Simple receiving

1. Whoever, without having previously ascertained its lawful origin, acquires or receives, under any title, a thing which, by its nature or by the quality of the person who holds it or offers it to him, or by the amount of the price or conditions of sale or offer, causes a reasonably diligent person to suspect that it comes from criminal conduct against the property of another person, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or with a fine.
2. Proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 272 Aggravated receiving

1. Whoever, with the intention of obtaining, for himself or for another person, patrimonial advantage, conceals a thing obtained by another person through a crime against property, receives it, pawns it, acquires it under any title, holds it, keeps it, transmits it or contributes to transmitting it, or in any other way secures, for himself or for a third party, its possession or the value or product directly resulting from it, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2. The offender is punished for the commission of the acts described in the preceding paragraph with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years, if:
a) He makes receiving a way of life or practices it habitually;
b) The goods, values or products have a value exceeding 2,000 US dollars;
c) If, at the time of receiving, the receiver had knowledge that the crime was committed with any of the circumstances referred to in article 253, paragraph 1.

###### Article 273 Assistance to the criminal

1. Whoever, after the commission of a crime against property, helps the offender of the crime to take advantage of the thing thus obtained or of a benefit directly resulting from the appropriated thing, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2. Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Article 274 Harmful administration

1. Whoever is entrusted with disposing of or administering the interests, services or patrimonial assets of others, even if he is a partner of the company or legal person to which those assets, interests or services belong, and by having intentionally infringed the rules of control and management or by having acted with serious violation of duties inherent to the function, causes economically significant patrimonial damage, is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 4 years.
2. If the assets, interests or services referred to in the preceding paragraph belong to the State, a legal person of public utility, a cooperative or popular association, the penalty of imprisonment from 2 to 6 years is applicable to the offender.
3. The same penalties are applicable to whoever appropriates or allows the unlawful appropriation of things which they could only dispose of within the scope and for the purposes proper to one who administers the property of others.

###### Artigo 275º Administração negligente

1.	Whoever, being in the conditions described in paragraph 1 of the previous article, causes serious patrimonial damage by not acting with the diligence to which according to the circumstances they were obliged and of which they were capable, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
2.	Imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine is applied if the assets or interests relate to any of the situations described in paragraph 2 of the previous article.
3.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

###### Artigo 276º

Falência ou insolvência intencional

1.	Whoever, by any means, leads a company to bankruptcy or places themselves in a situation of insolvency, with

the intention of harming creditors, if bankruptcy or insolvency is declared, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2.	If the facts described in the previous paragraph relate to public companies or cooperatives, the penalty is increased by one third in its minimum and maximum limits.

###### Artigo 277º

Falência ou insolvência negligente

1.	Whoever causes bankruptcy or insolvency through serious carelessness or imprudence, prodigality or manifestly excessive expenses, or through gross negligence in the exercise of their activity, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine, if bankruptcy or insolvency is declared.
2.	Criminal proceedings depend on a complaint.

#### TÍTULO V

DOS CRIMES CONTRA A REALIZAÇÃO DA JUSTIÇA

###### Artigo 278º

Falsidade de depoimento ou declaração

1.	Whoever gives testimony as a party, making false statements regarding facts about which they must testify, after having taken an oath and having been warned of the criminal consequences to which they are exposed by giving false testimony, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or with a fine.
2.	The same penalty is incurred by the suspect or accused regarding statements about identity and criminal record.
3.	If, as a consequence of the conduct described in the previous paragraphs, someone is deprived of liberty, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Artigo 279º

Falsidade de testemunho, perícia, interpretação ou tradução

1.	Whoever, as a witness, expert, technician, translator or interpreter, before a court or official competent to receive as evidence, testimony, report, information or translation, gives testimony, presents a report, gives information or makes false translations, is punished with imprisonment up to 4 years or with a fine.
2.	The same penalty is incurred by whoever, without just cause, refuses to testify or to present a report, information or translation.
3.	If the fact referred to in paragraph 1 is committed after the agent has taken an oath and has been warned of the criminal consequences to which they are exposed, the penalty is imprisonment up to 5 years.
4.	If, as a consequence of the conduct described in the previous paragraphs, someone is deprived of liberty, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Artigo 280º Equiparação à desistência

The repentance and retraction of the agent who has committed any of the facts described in the previous article before the effects of the falsity have been taken into account in the decision or have caused harm to another person, are equivalent to withdrawal.

###### Artigo 281º Suborno

1.	Whoever convinces or attempts to convince another person, through a gift or promise of patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage, to commit any of the facts referred to in articles 278º or 279º, is punished with imprisonment up to 4 years, when such practice occurs.
2.	If the commission of the facts referred to in articles 278º and 279º does not materialize, the agent is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine

###### Artigo 282º

Denegação de justiça
1.	The official who, within the scope of a procedural inquiry, jurisdictional proceedings, for administrative offences or disciplinary matters, knowingly decides or does not decide, promotes or does not promote, investigates or does not investigate or performs an act in the exercise of their functions, against the law, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	If the fact described in the previous paragraph is committed with the intention of harming or benefiting someone, the official is punished with imprisonment up to 5 years.
3.	If from the conduct described in the previous paragraphs results the deprivation of liberty of a person, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
4.	If the previous conduct is committed with gross negligence, the penalties are reduced by half in their minimum and maximum limits.

###### Artigo 283º Coacção sobre magistrado

1.	Whoever, by means of violence, threat of significant harm or by any other means, acts in a way to prevent a judge or public prosecutor from freely exercising their functions, is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 4 years.
2.	If the agent commits the facts taking advantage of being invested in a position of a political, public, military or police nature, the penalty is 2 to 8 years of imprisonment.
3.	If, as a consequence of the conduct described in the previous paragraphs, the magistrate omits or performs an act in violation of express law resulting in harm to third parties, the penalty is 3 to 10 years of imprisonment.

###### Artigo 284º

Obstrução à actividade jurisdicional

1.	Whoever, by any means, opposes, hinders or prevents the compliance or execution of a final judicial decision, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 5 years.

2.	If the agent who commits the facts described in the previous paragraphs exercises any political, public or military function which, by its nature, should inhibit them from committing such facts, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Artigo 285º Denúncia caluniosa

1.	Whoever, by any means, before an authority or publicly, with awareness of the falsity of the imputation, denounces or casts upon a certain person the suspicion of committing a crime, with the intention that criminal proceedings be instituted against them, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	If the false imputation refers to an administrative offence or disciplinary matter, the penalty shall be extraordinarily reduced.
3.	If the facts described in the previous paragraphs are deliberately promoted by any official charged with instituting the respective proceedings, the applicable penalties are increased by one third in their minimum and maximum limits.

###### Artigo 286º Não participação

Whoever, having knowledge of the commission of a public crime and, being obliged to report it, does not do so, is punished with the penalty corresponding to the crime they concealed reduced by two thirds in its minimum and maximum limits.

###### Artigo 287º

Prevaricação de magistrado ou funcionário

1.	The judge, public prosecutor or official who at any stage of jurisdictional proceedings, with the intention of benefiting or harming another person, performs any act within the scope of the functional powers they hold or omits an act they should perform, knowingly and against the law, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

2.	If from the facts described in the previous paragraph results the deprivation of liberty of any person or if they cause a situation of illegal imprisonment or detention, the penalty is 3 to 10 years of imprisonment.

###### Artigo 288º

Prevaricação de advogado ou defensor público
1.	The lawyer or public defender who intentionally harms a case entrusted to their representation is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 4 years.
2.	The lawyer or public defender who, in the same case, advocates or exercises the defense regarding persons whose interests are in conflict, with the intention of acting for the benefit or harm of any of them is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

###### Article 289 Simulation of crime

1. Whoever, without imputing it to a specific person, reports a crime or causes suspicion of its commission to be created before the competent authority, knowing that it did not occur, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2. If the act concerns a contravention, administrative offence or disciplinary infraction, the offender is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.
3. If the acts described in the preceding numbers are committed by officials charged with instituting the respective proceedings, the applicable penalties are aggravated by one third in their limits.

###### Article 290 Personal favouring

1. Whoever, totally or partially, prevents, frustrates or evades evidentiary or preventive activity of a competent authority, with the intention or awareness of preventing another person who committed a crime from being subjected to a penalty or security measure, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2. Attempt is punishable.

3. If the favouring is committed by an official who intervenes or has competence to intervene in the proceedings or who is charged with executing a penalty or security measure or with ordering its execution, the penalty is 2 to 5 years of imprisonment.
4. The commission of the acts described in number 1 is not punishable when:
a) The offender by the act seeks at the same time to prevent a penalty or security measure from being applied or executed against himself;
b) The offender is the spouse, descendant, ascendant, adopter or adoptee, relative or in-law up to the second degree of the person for whose benefit he acted or someone who lives with that person in a situation analogous to that of spouses.

###### Article 291

Violation of judicial secrecy

1. Whoever, in violation of a legal determination and without just cause, makes public the content of a criminal procedural act covered by judicial secrecy or in respect of which it has been decided to exclude publicity, is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 4 years.
2. If the violation is carried out by means of a media outlet, the penalty is 2 to 6 years of imprisonment.

#### TITLE VI

CRIMES COMMITTED IN THE EXERCISE OF PUBLIC FUNCTIONS

###### Article 292

Passive corruption for unlawful act

1. The official who, by himself or through an intermediary person, with his consent or ratification, solicits or accepts, for himself or for a third party, without it being due to him, a patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage, or its promise, for any act or omission contrary to the duties of office, even if prior to that solicitation or acceptance, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 15 years.

2. If the offender, before the commission of the act, voluntarily repudiates the offer or promise that he had accepted, or returns the advantage, or, in the case of a fungible thing, its value, he is exempted from penalty.
3. The penalty is specially mitigated if the offender concretely assists in the collection of decisive evidence for the identification or capture of other responsible parties.

###### Article 293 Passive corruption for lawful act

1. The official who, by himself or through an intermediary person, with his consent or ratification, solicits or accepts, for himself or for a third party, without it being due to him, a patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage, or its promise, for any act or omission not contrary to the duties of office, even if prior to that solicitation or acceptance, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or with a fine.
2. The same penalty is incurred by the official who, by himself or through an intermediary person, with his consent or ratification, solicits or accepts, for himself or for a third party, without it being due to him, a patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage from a person who before him has had, has or will have any claim dependent on the exercise of his public functions.

###### Article 294 Active corruption

1. Whoever, by himself or through an intermediary person with his consent or ratification, gives or promises to an official, or to a third party with the knowledge of the official, a patrimonial or non-patrimonial advantage that is not due to the official, with the purpose indicated in Article 292, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.
2. If the purpose of the conduct described in the preceding number is that indicated in Article 293, the offender is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or with a fine.

###### Article 295 Embezzlement

1. The official who unlawfully appropriates for his own benefit or that of another person, money or movable property, public or private, that is delivered to him, is in his possession or is accessible to him by reason of his functions, is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.
2. If the official lends, pledges or, in any way, encumbers the assets or objects referred to in the preceding number, he is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.
3. If the assets or objects referred to in the preceding numbers exceed 5,000 US dollars, the penalties are, respectively, imprisonment from 4 to 12 years or from 2 to 5 years.
4. If the assets or objects referred to in number 1 are of a value less than 50 US dollars, the offender is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or with a fine.

###### Article 296 Embezzlement of use

The official who makes use or permits another person to make use, for purposes other than those for which they are intended, of vehicles or other movable things of significant value, that are delivered to him, are in his possession or are accessible to him by reason of his functions, to obtain, for himself or for a third party, unlawful benefit or cause harm to someone, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years, if those things are in his possession or are accessible to him by reason of his functions.

###### Article 297 Abuse of power

The official who abuses powers or violates duties inherent to his functions, with the intention of obtaining for himself or for a third party, unlawful benefit or causing harm to

another person, is punished with imprisonment from 1 to 4 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable by virtue of another legal provision.

###### Article 298

Abusive employment of public force
The official who, being competent to use, requisition or order the employment of public force, does so to prevent the execution of the law, of a regular warrant of justice or of a lawful order of a public authority, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years, if a more severe penalty is not applicable to him by another legal provision.

###### Article 299 Economic participation in business

1. The official who, by reason of the exercise of public office, must intervene in a contract or other operation or activity, and takes advantage of that condition, to obtain for himself or for a third party, directly or through an intermediary person, a patrimonial advantage, or, in any other way, unlawful economic participation and thereby harms the public interests that he should administer, supervise, defend or realize, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2. If from the preceding conduct there result losses to the State exceeding 10,000 US dollars, the penalty of imprisonment is from 3 to 15 years.

###### Article 300 Refusal of cooperation

The official who, having been lawfully requested by the competent authority to provide due cooperation for the administration of justice or any other public service, and refuses to provide it or without justified reason does not provide it, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or with a fine.

###### Article 301 Aggravation

1. When the crimes referred to in this Title are committed by holders of political offices or by magistrates, the penalties prescribed are increased by one third in their maximum limit.
2. The non-penal and procedural effects relating to the situations referred to in the preceding number are established in special legislation.

###### Article 302 Concept of public official

1. For the purposes of criminal law, the term public official encompasses:
a) The civil servant;
b) The administrative agent;
c) Members of the armed forces and police;
d) Whoever, even provisionally or temporarily, whether remunerated or gratuitously, voluntarily or compulsorily, has been called to perform or participate in the performance of an activity comprised in the administrative or jurisdictional public function, or, in the same circumstances, performs functions in public utility bodies or participates in them.
e) The foreign public official who holds a legislative, executive, administrative or judicial office in a foreign country, already designated or invested, or a person who exercises a public function for a foreign country, including in a public body or a public enterprise.
f) The official of a public international organization whom such organization has authorized to act on its behalf
2. For criminal purposes, the provisions of this title apply to whoever performs political, governmental or legislative functions.

#### TITLE VII

CRIMES OF FALSIFICATION

##### CHAPTER I FALSIFICATION OF DOCUMENTS

###### Article 303

Falsification of document or technical notation

1. Whoever, with intent to cause harm to another person or to the State, or to obtain for himself or for another person, unlawful benefit:
a) Manufactures false document or technical notation, falsifies or alters a document or abuses another person's signature to prepare a false document;
b) Causes to be falsely recorded in a document or technical notation a legally relevant fact;
c) Falsely certifies, based on professional, technical or scientific knowledge, about the physical or mental state or quality of a person, animals or things;
d) Uses any of the documents or technical notations referred to in the preceding paragraphs, manufactured or falsified or issued by another;
is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2. The disruptive action on technical or automatic devices by means of which the results of the notation are influenced is equivalent to falsification of technical notation.
3. The attempt is punishable.

###### Article 304 Aggravated falsification

1. If the facts referred to in number 1 of the preceding article concern an authentic document or one with equal force, a sealed will, a postal order, a bill of exchange, a cheque, other commercial documents transferable by endorsement or a technical notation relating to the identification, in part or in whole, of motor vehicles, aircraft or boats, the agent is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.
2. If the facts described in the preceding number or in number
1 of article 309 are committed by a public official in

the exercise of his functions, the penalty is 2 to 8 years of imprisonment.

###### Article 305 Falsification of public document

The public official who in the exercise of his functions, with intent to cause harm to another person or to the State, or to obtain for himself or for another person unlawful benefit:
a) Omits a fact which the document to which the law attributes public faith is intended to certify or authenticate; or
b) Inserts an act or document in a protocol, register or official book without complying with legal formalities;
is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

###### Article 306

Use of another's identification document

Whoever, with intent to cause harm to another person or to the State, uses an identification document of which another person is the holder, is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

##### CHAPTER II FALSIFICATION OF CURRENCY

###### Article 307 Counterfeiting of currency

1. Whoever commits counterfeiting of currency or depreciates legitimate metallic currency, with intent to put it into circulation as genuine is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.
2. If the agent, in addition to committing the facts described in the preceding number, actually places the currency into circulation, the penalty is aggravated by one third at the maximum limit.
3. Whoever, by agreement with the counterfeiter, exposes for sale, puts into circulation or by any other means disseminates the currency is punished with imprisonment from 3 to 10 years.

###### Article 308 Passing of false currency

Whoever, outside the cases referred to in the preceding article, acquires in order to put into circulation or actually puts into circulation, sells or by any means disseminates the counterfeited or depreciated currency, as if it were genuine, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

###### Article 309

Counterfeiting of sealed or stamped values

1. Whoever, in order to sell them, use them or by any other means put them into circulation as legitimate, commits counterfeiting or falsification of sealed or stamped values whose manufacture and supply belongs exclusively to the Timorese State, is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.
2. Whoever commits the facts described in relation to postage stamps or any other means of postal franking in use by the Post Office of Timor-Leste is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
3. The use of the falsified products referred to in the preceding numbers, knowing them to be falsified, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
4. The attempt is punishable.

###### Article 310

Counterfeiting of seals, stamps, marks or official stamps

1. Whoever, with intent to use them as authentic or intact, acquires, counterfeits or falsifies seals, stamps, marks or official stamps of any authority or public office is punished with imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.
2. Whoever uses the objects referred to in the preceding number knowing them to be falsified or without authorization from the rightful person, to cause harm to another person or to the State, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

3. If whoever uses the said objects is the counterfeiter himself, the penalty of number 1 is increased by one third at the maximum limit.
4. The attempt to commit the facts described in number 2 is punishable.

###### Article 311 Weights and measures

1. Whoever, with intent to harm another person or the State, falsifies or by any other means alters or uses after such acts have been committed, weights, measures, scales or other measuring instruments, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2. The attempt is punishable.

###### Article 312 Seizure and forfeiture

Counterfeited, falsified or differentiated currency, and equivalent objects, as well as weights, measures or any and all instruments intended for the commission of the crimes provided for in this Title are seized and put out of use or destroyed.

#### TITLE VIII

CRIMES AGAINST THE ECONOMY

###### Artigo 313º Money laundering

1.	Whoever, knowing that the goods or products originate from the commission of crimes, in any form of participation, of terrorism, of trafficking in arms or nuclear products, of trafficking or sale of persons, of pornography involving minors, of corruption, of fraud or extortion, of tax fraud, of trafficking in protected species and trafficking in human organs or tissues or of other serious crimes, whose minimum limit of the penalty is greater than 4 years of imprisonment:

a)	Converts, transfers, assists or facilitates any operation of conversion or transfer of such goods or products, in whole or in part, directly or indirectly, with the purpose of concealing or disguising their illicit origin or of helping a person involved in the commission of any of these offences to evade the legal consequences of their acts; or
b)	Conceals or disguises the true nature, origin, location, disposition, movement or properties of such goods or products or rights relating to them;
c)	Acquires or receives such advantages in any capacity or uses or holds or retains them,
is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from 4 to 12 years of imprisonment.
2.	The punishment of the acts provided for in paragraphs a) to c) of the preceding number takes place even if the facts that constitute the underlying crime were committed outside the national territory or even if the place of commission of the act or the identity of the perpetrators is unknown.
3.	The knowledge, intention or purpose, required as constituent elements of the crime may be recognised from actual and concrete factual circumstances.
4.	For the demonstration and proof of the illicit origin of the income, prior conviction of the agent for the commission of the underlying crime is not necessary.
5.	The underlying offence includes crimes committed outside the national territory, when the acts are constituted as a crime in the state where they were committed and within the territory of Timor-Leste.
6.	The crime of money laundering does not apply to persons who commit the underlying crime.
7.	Attempted money laundering is punishable and may be subject to a reduction of penalty under the general terms.
8.	The punishment provided for the commission of the typical unlawful acts described in paragraphs a) to c) of number 1 must not exceed the maximum limit provided for the corresponding underlying offences.

###### Artigo 314º Tax fraud

1.	Whoever, in order not to pay or to allow a third party not to pay, totally or partially, any tax, fee or other pecuniary fiscal obligation owed to the State, by:
a)	Not declaring the facts subject to taxation or those necessary for its assessment;
b)	Incorrectly declaring the facts on which taxation is based; or
c)	Preventing by any means or concealing the elements necessary for a correct inspection of the activity or facts subject to taxation.
is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.
2.	If the amount owed and not paid is greater than 5,000 US dollars, the agent is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from 2 to 8 years.

###### Artigo 315º

Illicit import and export of goods or merchandise

1.	Whoever exports or imports, without a licence, goods or merchandise whose export or import, by legal determination, is dependent on a licence from any entity, or without passing through customs, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	If the acts are committed with negligence the penalty is imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### Artigo 316º Smuggling

1.	Whoever imports or exports goods or merchandise without conducting them through customs or other mandatory passage controls for entry or exit of goods or merchandise from the territory of Timor-Leste, is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from 2 to 6 years or a fine.
2.	If the value of the goods or merchandise is greater than
10,000 US dollars or if the agent commits the

acts described in the preceding number habitually, the penalty is imprisonment from 2 to 8 years or a fine.
3.	If the conduct referred to in the preceding numbers concerns goods or merchandise subject to a licence or whose import or export is prohibited, the penalties referred to in the preceding numbers are aggravated by one third in their limits.

###### Artigo 317º Customs evasion

1.	Whoever imports or exports goods or merchandise, evading totally or partially, the payment of customs duties or fees owed for the entry or exit of those is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from 1 to 4 years or a fine.
2.	If the value of the goods or merchandise is greater than
10,000 US dollars or if the agent commits the acts described in the preceding number habitually, the penalty is imprisonment from 2 to 6 years or a fine.

###### Artigo 318º Exemption from penalty

The agent of the commission of the acts described in the preceding articles may be exempted from penalty whenever they voluntarily pay the customs duties or fees that are assessed and the conduct is occasional.

###### Artigo 319º

Irregular administration of public funds

1.	Whoever gives public funds an application different from that legally established, is punished with imprisonment up to 2 years or a fine.
2.	If the destination of the irregular application is not carried out for a public purpose, this being the legally established purpose, the penalty is imprisonment from 2 to 6 years.

###### Artigo 320º Disobedience to requisition of goods

1.	Whoever does not comply with the requisition, ordered by the Government, of goods considered indispensable for the supply of economic activities or for public consumption, is punished with a penalty of imprisonment from 1 to 4 years or a fine.
2.	Negligent conduct is punished with a penalty of imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### Artigo 321º

Destruction of goods relevant to the economy

1.	Whoever by any means, destroys, damages or renders unusable their own goods of relevant interest to the national economy or in any other way removes them from compliance with legal duties imposed by reason of the national economy is punished with a penalty of imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.
2.	Negligent conduct is punished with imprisonment up to 1 year or a fine.

###### Artigo 322º Illicit exploitation of gambling

1.	Whoever, in any form, operates games of fortune or chance outside legally authorised places and without due legal authorisation, or ensures the outcome through error, deception or use of any equipment, is punished with a penalty of imprisonment up to 3 years or with a penalty of a fine.
2.	Games of fortune or chance are those whose result is uncertain because it is based exclusively or fundamentally on luck.

###### Article 323 Disruption of public act

Whoever, with the intention of preventing or prejudicing the results of a judicial auction or against a public auction or tender, manages, by means of a gift, promise, violence or threat, to ensure that someone does not bid or does not compete or that, even while bidding and winning, they

do so under conditions of lack of freedom in the practice of those acts, is punished with imprisonment up to 3 years or a fine.

Law 13/2008, of 13 October Legislative authorisation in criminal matters

The constitutional mechanism of legislative authorisation is provided for in Article 96(1)(a) of the Constitution of the Democratic Republic of Timor-Leste.

The choice of this mechanism, which the Constitution provides for, enables greater speed in the legislative process, with Parliament defining the broad lines of legislative policy that should inform the final instrument, referring to the Government the harmonisation of aspects of legal technique, an intervention subordinate to the directives emanating from the National Parliament. The separation of powers attributed to the various constitutional bodies is observed, with regard to the exercise of legislative power.

This law of legislative authorisation sets out the content and scope in terms that guarantee respect for the fundamental rights, freedoms and guarantees of citizens, within the scope of Criminal Law. Moreover, the proposed solutions take into consideration the general principles of criminal law enshrined in international legal instruments, already ratified by Timor-Leste, as well as the social and cultural context of the country.

It should also be noted that the content of the legislative authorisation now granted is articulated with the Code of Criminal Procedure and other complementary legislation approved and in force in the meantime.

The approval has as a future consequence the abandonment of the subsidiary application in Timor-Leste of the Criminal Code of Indonesia, also requiring the revocation or harmonisation of legislative instruments subsequent to 20 May 2002, which contain provisions relating to the definition of crimes, penalties or security measures.

Thus, the National Parliament decrees, pursuant to Articles 95(1) and 96 of the Constitution of the Republic, to have force as law, the following:

###### Article 1 Object

Authorisation is granted to the Government to approve the Criminal Code and revoke the legislation in force in this matter, namely with regard to the definition of crimes, penalties, security measures and their respective prerequisites.


This excerpt sits under: Cláudio Ximenes > BOOK II SPECIAL PART > TITLE VIII

###### Article 2 Meaning and scope

1. The Penal Code drafted under this legislative authorisation law shall observe the constitutional principles and norms and the norms contained in international instruments that, in this matter, bind the Timorese State.
2. The authorisation has the following meaning and scope:
a) Construction of a criminal legal system aimed at protecting legal interests essential to life in Timorese society and the reintegration of offenders therein;
b) Enshrine the principle of legality and the prohibition of analogy with regard to the application of criminal law, although with the possibility of applying law subsequent to the commission of the crime whenever it proves more favourable in the specific case;
c) Stipulate that exceptional or temporary laws apply to acts committed during their validity even if judged after the period of validity;
d) Establish norms that define the place and time of commission of the crime;
e) Enshrine the principle of territoriality for the application of Timorese criminal law, individualising the restrictions to such principle and the cases of application to situations occurring outside Timorese territory;
f) Regulate the general prerequisites of crime, equating omission to action, unless the intention of the law is otherwise, whenever the legal type of crime comprises a certain result and the agent has the duty to prevent it;
g) Make objective imputation dependent on the existence of a causal nexus between the conduct and the typical result;
h) Objective imputation shall depend on the verification of intent or negligence, defining the types of intent (direct, necessary and eventual) and the modalities of negligence (conscious and unconscious);
i) Provide that criminal liability for crimes provided for in the Penal Code is non-transferable and has as its sole agents natural persons, admitting situations of liability for acting on behalf of another and that special legislation may enshrine the criminal liability of legal persons;
j) Enshrine the non-imputability of minors under 16 years of age and the requirements for non-imputability by reason of mental abnormality;
k) Admit the possibility of aggravation of the penalty whenever the liability of the agent, at least on grounds of negligence, must encompass a result not comprised in the type;
l) Enshrine error as to circumstances as excluding intent and error as to unlawfulness as relevant to exclude culpability;
m) As to the forms of crime, establish as a principle the non-punishability of preparatory acts, and the rule of punishment of attempt in intentional crimes punishable with more than 3 years' imprisonment;
n) Define the concept of attempt, the measure of its punishment, cases of impossible attempt, the relevance of voluntary desistance and subsequent repentance both in individual actions and in cases of participation;
o) Establish the forms of authorship, instigation or complicity and define the liability of participants in culpability and unlawfulness;
p) Provide for and define the prerequisites of situations of concurrence of crimes, concurrence of sanctions, concurrence of crime with another offence and concurrence of norms;
q) Establish the rules for punishment of concurrence of crimes, even when subsequently discovered, admitting the possibility that the cumulation may reach 30 years' imprisonment and 600 days' fine;
r) Define continued crime and the rules for its punishment with the penalty applicable to the most serious conduct;
s) Enshrine the causes of exclusion of unlawfulness and culpability, systematically distinguishing one from the other and defining the respective prerequisites for application;
t) Introduce a title relating to circumstances which, not forming part of the legal type, assume relevance in determining the criminal liability of the agent;
u) Proceed to the enumeration of aggravating circumstances of a general nature and to the enumeration of mitigating circumstances;
v) Provide for a general clause of extraordinary mitigation beyond the special cases provided for in the law and delimit the respective consequences in determining the penal framework, through a model of degrees of mitigation;
w) As general modifying aggravating circumstances enshrine recidivism and criminal habituality providing for the respective prerequisites and consequences in the domain of penalty;
x) Construct a system of penalties and security measures whose difference is based on the capacity or not of culpability of the agent of the act; in the first case the concrete penalty may never exceed the measure of culpability and in the second they must be based on the dangerousness of the agent;
y) Enunciate the determining principles in the choice of penalty and security measure as well as the effects thereof and the counting of time of deprivation of liberty before conviction;
z) With regard to the execution of penalties and measures depriving liberty, provide for the possibility of benefit of conditional release and the regime for probation, always with the consent of the person concerned and with the other requirements to be enshrined in complementary legislation which may provide for the conditions of execution of the prison sentence, the rights and duties of prisoners and the existence of open regimes;
aa) Create a system of criminal sanctions for those imputable based on the following principal penalties: imprisonment, fine, community service and admonition;
bb) The penalty of imprisonment with a minimum duration of 30 days and maximum of 30 years, with the possibility of being suspended in its execution whenever it is not applied in a measure exceeding 3 years and the requirements of prevention of future crimes do not require its enforcement, and may or may not be conditioned on compliance with some duties and/or subject to monitoring by reintegration services during the period of suspension;
cc) Regulate the possibility of modification of the suspension regime initially applied and its revocation if during the suspension period the convicted person commits an intentional crime for which he is convicted with a prison sentence or in cases of intentional non-compliance with the duties to which he is subject;
dd) Enshrine a regime of substitution of prison sentences applied in a measure not exceeding 12 months by fine or community service, according to the circumstances, with the duty of the court to justify the non-substitution when, being able to, it does not use such measure;
ee) Not make alternative imprisonment correspond to the substitute fine and, in case of unjustified non-payment thereof, impose compliance with the imprisonment initially applied;
ff) Stipulate that the fine penalty is always fixed in time, at a minimum of 10 days and maximum of 360 days, in principle, and determine that each day of fine corresponds to an amount to be fixed between half a dollar and 200 dollars, to be fixed according to the economic and financial situation of the convicted person;
gg) Whenever a fine penalty is directly applied, stipulate the duty to make alternative imprisonment correspond thereto for the corresponding time reduced to 2/3, as well as the possibility of payment of the fine in instalments, its reduction or exemption in cases of great supervening difficulty or impossibility of compliance therewith;
hh) Provide for the possibility of the penalty of community service being able to substitute both imprisonment and fine in situations of minor and medium criminality and establish some of the conditions for its application and execution in coordination with reintegration services, as well as cases of exemption or reduction, leaving to complementary law the regulation of the other conditions of application and operation of this penalty;
ii) Enshrine the penalty of admonition for cases not punishable with abstract prison sentence exceeding 3 years provided that, cumulatively, reparation of the damage is verified, the agent is a first-time offender and the needs of prevention and rehabilitation are satisfied with the admonition;
jj) Establish a system of accessory penalties, making certain crimes correspond to the prohibition of the exercise of rights and professions, namely the temporary suspension of public functions, prohibition of the exercise of function, prohibition of driving, revocation of weapon carrying licence and expulsion of foreigners; such accessory penalties shall not be of automatic operation depending on the prior application of a principal penalty and their duration variable with the measure of culpability;
kk) To those declared non-imputable by virtue of mental abnormality enshrine the possibility of application of security measures, of limited temporal duration, namely internment whenever reasons of dangerousness so advise and the obligation thereof to cease when the state that legitimised it ends, admitting that in the case of foreigners it may be replaced by expulsion;
ll) Also provide for the existence of measures of professional interdiction, revocation of licence and prohibition of driving for those non-imputable by reason of mental abnormality;
mm) Create an autonomous chapter relating to the matter of determination of the concrete penalty, both imprisonment and fine, enunciating the general principles and the operations necessary for its choice and quantification;
nn) Stipulate that compensation for losses and damages arising from the commission of a crime, as to prerequisites and calculation, is governed by the norms of civil law but, unless otherwise declared by the injured party, shall be officially awarded by the court in the criminal proceedings and that the credit resulting therefrom is privileged;
oo) Determine the conditions for the loss of objects and advantages consequent upon a crime to be decreed by the court;
pp) Define for purposes of the exercise of the right of complaint crimes as public or semi-public, identify the holders of that right, the time limit for its exercise and the conditions of waiver or withdrawal thereof;
qq) With regard to the extinction of criminal liability, fix the time limits for prescription of criminal proceedings taking into consideration the different frameworks of the type of crimes, the form of counting and the causes of suspension of prescription;
rr) Fix the time limits for prescription of penalties, security measures and accessory penalties, as well as situations of suspension thereof;
ss) Regulate the other cases of extinction of liability such as the death of the agent, amnesty, general pardon and pardon;
tt) Enshrine the imprescriptibility of criminal proceedings and penalties for the commission of crimes of war, against peace, humanity and freedom;
uu) Organise the special part of the Penal Code, Book Two, grouping the types of crimes according to the protected legal interests, creating Titles and Chapters as homogeneous as possible;
vv) In Title I, group the unlawful acts concerning crimes of Genocide and against Humanity, crimes of War, including the prohibitive conducts of certain methods and means referenced in international law, war crimes against property and against goods protected by distinctive insignia or emblems or other rights and, still in this Title, the so-called crimes against Peace and Freedom, namely prohibiting incitement to war, conducts of racial or religious discrimination, trafficking and slavery of persons and enshrining a legal type of crime that encompasses situations of terrorism;
ww) Still in Title I of the Special Part, the future Penal Code of Timor-Leste must take into account the international obligations assumed by the Country upon subscribing to and ratifying the Statute of the International Criminal Court;
xx) In Title II of the Special Part of the Code, eminently personal legal interests shall be protected, highlighting life, physical integrity, personal freedom, honour and protection of private life;
yy) Structure the protection of the legal interest of life through the imposition of differentiated penal frameworks according to the type of culpability of the agent (intent and negligence) and the qualifying circumstances of the greater or lesser degree of censurability namely according to motives, the quality of the victim and the means and methods used to carry out the criminal design;
zz) Enshrine the protection of uterine life; provide for the privileging of infanticidal conducts by the parturient to restrictive cases occurring during childbirth, by disturbing influence thereof; criminalise incitement to suicide and situations of intentional abandonment or exposure of another that places their life in danger;
aaa) Define a set of incriminating norms that prohibit behaviours violating the physical integrity of another person, graduating the abstract penal framework based on the degree of unlawfulness and culpability of the agent manifested in the seriousness of the injury, the type of culpability and the possibility or not of a certain result that exceeds the criminal design formulated still supporting the imputation, at least on grounds of negligence, what doctrine calls aggravation by result;
bbb) Distinguish the incrimination of offences by means of poisonous substances, reciprocal offences and participation in affray with two or more persons;
ccc) Lastly, still within the scope of bodily offences, implement two legal types of crime that specifically provide for the punishment of ill-treatment of minors or incapable persons and of spouses or those cohabiting in an analogous situation;
ddd) In the chapter on protection of personal freedom criminalise conducts traditionally and at the level of comparative law integrating the types of crime of threats, coercion, kidnapping, abduction and other acts violating individual freedom and, specifically, create the type of unlawful act relating to the sale of persons and the omission of denunciation of situations capable of constituting the crimes in question;
eee) Enshrine a chapter relating to sexual criminality highlighting the legal interest of freedom in this domain as determinant of the conducts to be criminalised, protecting the youngest from acts for which they are not yet prepared to consciously decide in freedom, incriminate conducts revealing sexual exploitation and the most common forms of sexual abuse whether by fraud or by reason of age;
fff) In a very particular way incriminate the practices of sexual prostitution, child pornography and any acts of a sexual nature with minors under twelve years of age;
ggg) Introduce the criminalisation of violation of secrecy, violation of domicile and other places and violation of correspondence and telecommunications;
hhh) Group in a single title a set of crimes that protect legal interests relating to public peace and tranquillity, the security of the State, life in society, against public authority and within the scope of electoral activities;
