n) To define the concept of attempt, the punishment of attempt up to where it exists, cases in which there is impossible attempt, voluntary desistance and subsequent repentance, in singular action or in participation; o) To establish the various forms of authorship, instigation or complicity, and to define the responsibility of participants in culpability and illicitness; p) To provide for and define the presuppositions for situations in which multiple crimes are in concurrence, multiple sanctions are in concurrence, crimes are in concurrence with another infraction and multiple norms are in concurrence; q) To establish rules concerning punishment for crimes in concurrence, even with supervening knowledge, and also to admit that accumulation may give rise to imprisonment for up to 30 years and a fine for up to 600 days; r) To define continued crime and rules concerning its punishment with the penalty applicable to the most serious conduct; s) To consecrate causes which exclude illicitness and culpability, and to systematically autonomize each and to define the presuppositions for the application of each; t) To include in one title the various circumstances which, even if not entering into the legal type, have relevance to determine the agent's criminal responsibility; u) To enumerate159 general aggravating circumstances and also general attenuating circumstances; v) To provide for a general clause concerning extraordinary attenuation, together with special cases provided for in law and to delimit their consequences in the determination of the penal framework, with a model which includes various degrees of attenuation; w) As a general modifying aggravating circumstance, to consecrate criminal recidivism and habitual criminality with their presuppositions and consequences for the penalty; x) To establish a system concerning penalties and security measures and to base their difference on whether the agent who committed the act has capacity or not to receive culpability; in the first case the concrete penalty may not be more serious than the agent's culpability and in the second case it must be based on the agent's dangerousness; y) To enunciate determining160 principles to choose the penalty and security measure and also determining concerning the effect of penalties and security measures and concerning the counting of time in which there is deprivation of liberty before conviction; z) Concerning the execution of penalties and measures depriving liberty, to provide for the possibility to grant conditional release and probation regime, when, with the consent of the convicted person and with other requirements to be set out in complementary legislation which shall provide for conditions

159 Enumerate (v) – To make a list. To enumerate aggravating circumstances = To include in a list the aggravating circumstances. 160 Determining (adj) – Fundamental; which serves as a basis; which serves as a foundation; which has great influence. for the execution of imprisonment, the rights and obligations of prisoners and also open regime; aa) To establish a system of criminal sanctions for imputables based on the following principal penalties: imprisonment, fine, community service and admonishment; bb) Imprisonment with its minimum duration of 30 days and maximum of 30 years, with the possibility to suspend its execution when the penalty applied does not exceed 3 years and the fulfillment of such penalty is not necessary to prevent future crime, with or without conditions to fulfill some duty and/or to enter into accompaniment for reinsertion during the period of suspension; cc) To regulate the possibility to modify the suspension regime newly applied and the revocation of suspension when during the period of suspension the convicted person commits an intentional crime giving rise to imprisonment or in the case where there is intentional non-fulfillment of the obligation falling upon him or her; dd) To consecrate a regime concerning the substitution of imprisonment applied not exceeding 12 months with a fine or community service, according to the circumstances, with the obligation of the court to justify non-substitution in situations where, although possible, such measure is not applied; ee) Not to give correspondence between alternative imprisonment and substitutive fine and, when there is unjustified non-payment of such fine, to give the obligation to apply the newly applied imprisonment; ff) To stipulate that the fine penalty must be fixed by time, with its minimum of 10 days and its maximum of 360 days, as a rule, and to determine that each day of fine shall have correspondence with an amount, with its minimum of half a dollar and its maximum of 200 dollars, which shall be fixed according to the convicted person's economic and financial situation; gg) When directly applying a fine penalty, to stipulate the obligation to give correspondence to such fine with alternative imprisonment for a corresponding time reduced by 2/3, and also the possibility to pay the fine by installment, reduction or exemption of such fine in cases

where there is supervening great difficulty or impossibility to pay such fine; hh) To provide for the possibility to place a community service penalty in place of imprisonment or fine penalty in situations concerning petty criminality and medium criminality and to establish conditions for its application and execution according to coordination with reinsertion services, and also cases concerning exemption or reduction, and to leave to complementary law to regulate other conditions concerning the application and functioning of this penalty; ii) To consecrate admonishment penalty for cases not punishable with abstract imprisonment exceeding 3 years, namely, cumulatively, where there is reparation for damage, the agent is a primary delinquent and admonishment is sufficient to achieve prevention and recovery; jj) To establish a system of accessory penalties, in which, for certain crimes, the exercise of rights and professions shall be prohibited, namely, temporary suspension from public function, prohibition to exercise function, prohibition on driving, cancellation of license to hold firearms and expulsion of foreigners; these accessory penalties do not function automatically but are only applied when a principal penalty is applied and their measure varies according to the measure of culpability; kk) To consecrate for those already declared inimputable due to psychic anomaly the possibility to apply security measures, with limited temporal duration, namely internment when dangerousness advises it and also to consecrate that the obligation to apply such measure ends when the state giving rise to such measure ends, and for foreigners expulsion may be placed in place of such measure; ll) To also provide for inimputables due to psychic anomaly professional interdiction measures, cancellation of license and prohibition on driving; mm) To establish an autonomous chapter concerning the determination of concrete penalty, for imprisonment and for fine, indicating general principles and operations necessary for the choice and quantification of such penalty; nn) To establish that civil law regulates indemnification for loss and damage arising from the commission of crime, and also its presuppositions and calculation, but, except for a different declaration by the injured party, the court shall award indemnification ex officio and the credit arising from this shall have privilege; oo) To determine the conditions giving rise to the court to declare forfeited objects or advantages arising from crime; pp) To define, as a criterion to exercise the right to complaint, various crimes as public or semi-public, to identify the holder of this right, the time limit to exercise this right and conditions for renunciation and desistance of this right; qq) Concerning the extinction of criminal responsibility, to fix the time limit for the prescription of criminal proceedings, taking into account the various frameworks of these various crimes, how to count and the causes for suspension of prescription; rr) To fix the time limit for the prescription of penalties, security measures and accessory penalties, and also situations giving rise to suspension of prescription; ss) To regulate other extinctions of responsibility, due to the death of the agent, due to amnesty, due to generic pardon and due to pardon; tt) To consecrate imprescriptibility for criminal proceedings and penalties for war crimes, crimes against peace, humanity and liberty; uu) To organize the special part of the Criminal Code, book II, in which the various types of crime are included according to the legal good protected in titles and chapters as homogeneous as possible; vv) In title I to include illicit acts concerning the crimes of genocide and against humanity, war crimes, including prohibited conducts using methods and means referred to in international law, war crimes against property and against goods protected with distinctive insignia or emblem or other rights, and, also in this title, crimes called crimes against peace and liberty, namely to prohibit incitement to war, conduct of racial or religious discrimination, trafficking and slavery

of persons and also to consecrate a type of crime concerning terrorism; ww) In title I of this future Criminal Code of Timor-Leste the general part must also pay attention to the international obligations which the Country has already accepted when subscribing to and ratifying the Statute of the International Criminal Court; xx) In title II of the special part eminently personal legal goods shall be protected, such as life, physical integrity, personal liberty, honor and protection of private life; yy) To structure the protection of the legal good life with provision for various penal frameworks according to what type of culpability the agent has (intent and negligence) and circumstances which qualify the degree of censurability as lesser or greater, namely according to motive, the quality of the victim and the means and method used to realize the criminal design; zz) To consecrate protection for uterine life; to provide as privileged the conduct of an infanticidal parturient in limited cases occurring during childbirth due to the disturbing influence of childbirth; to criminalize incitement to suicide and abandonment or intentional exposure of another person which creates danger to life; aaa) To define a broad incriminating norm which prohibits conduct violating the physical integrity of another person, with graduation of the abstract penal framework according to the agent's illicitness and culpability indicated in the seriousness of the injury, according to the type of culpability and according to whether the result exceeding the criminal design formulated may give rise to imputation or not, at least for negligence - what doctrine calls aggravation by result; bbb) To autonomize incrimination for offense with poisonous substance, reciprocal offense and participation in a fight with two or more persons; ccc) In the context of bodily offense, a legal type of crime shall be implemented which provides for punishment for ill-treatment of minors or incapacitated persons and for spouse or person living as husband and wife; ddd) In the chapter concerning personal liberty, conduct which, traditionally and according to comparative law, integrates the types of crime of threat, coercion, kidnapping, abduction, and other acts violating individual liberty and, specifically, to create a typical illicit act concerning trafficking in persons and omission of denunciation concerning situations which may constitute these crimes; eee) To consecrate a chapter on sexual criminality, with emphasis on the legal good of liberty in this area as determinant for the conduct to be criminalized, in order to protect those who are still younger from acts for which they do not yet have preparation to, according to their conscience, decide freely, to incriminate conduct constituting the most common forms of sexual exploitation and sexual abuse, such as with fraud or due to age; fff) To especially incriminate practices constituting sexual prostitution, child pornography and other sexual acts with minors under 12 years of age; ggg) To include criminalization for violation of secrecy, violation of domicile and other places and violation of correspondence and telecommunications; hhh) To bring together in one title crimes which protect legal goods concerning public peace and tranquility, State security, life in society, against public authority and in the context of electoral activity; iii) Concerning conduct which may violate public peace and tranquility, to prevent criminal association, public instigation to commit crime, participation in public riot, conduct preventing citizens from exercising political rights, disobedience to legitimate order to disperse, to criminalize practices constituting trafficking in influence and also to punish abuse of public signs or uniforms; jjj) Concerning the internal security of the State, to provide for incriminating norms for conduct which may actually create danger to such security, namely to consecrate as legal types of crime treason against the homeland, collaboration with enemy armed forces of the Timorese State, sabotage of national defense and campaign against peace efforts in Timor-Leste, attack against the Head of State or other constitutional organs or conduct seeking to alter the State according to law, diplomatic infidelity161, violation of State secrecy and outrage162 to Timorese national symbols; kkk) To criminalize fraudulent conduct in electoral activity regardless of its agent and the phase of the electoral process, obstruction to exercise candidacy or exercise of illegal candidacy, electoral propaganda against the respective legal framework163 and actions disturbing the normal development of electoral acts; lll) Life in modern society develops together with conduct which may give rise to danger with more or less proximate concretization and may violate goods whose integrity must be guaranteed, such as driving motor vehicles, use or possession of weapons and activity with relevance to citizen and public health; therefore some types of crime concerning this matter must be provided for; mmm) Thus also conduct violating religious values implanted in Timorese society, non-fulfillment of alimentary obligation and other obligations concerning the custody of minors, of solidarity in calamity or disaster or obligations falling upon certain professional activities must be criminalized; nnn) To criminally sanction conduct unsustainably harmful164 to the environment, namely fauna, flora, natural habitat and resources in the Timorese subsoil; ooo) To criminally sanction conduct against the exercise of public authority, namely with acts constituting disobedience, obstruction, destruction, resistance or usurpation of public office; ppp) Concerning the executing action of penalties and security measures removing liberty, to criminalize conduct constituting removal of prisoner, evasion,

161 Infidelity (n) – Treason; violation of trust
162 Outrage (n) – Offense; fault. 163 Framework (n) – Disposition. Legal framework = legal disposition; norm. 164 Harmful (adj) – Which harms or causes prejudice

assistance by official for evasion and riot of prisoners; qqq) To protect patrimonial legal goods to consecrate in one chapter crimes against property and in another chapter crimes against patrimony; rrr) To establish a normative system protecting property based on legal types of crime used for a long time in other penal legislations, such as theft, robbery, breach of trust and damage, and to structure these types as simple and aggravated, according to circumstances such as the value and nature of the thing appropriated, the means used, how the action is carried out, violence, threat or coercion or other circumstances which may significantly increase culpability and illicitness; sss) Concerning property, norms typifying theft for use of vehicle, violence following subtraction, usurpation of immovable property and alteration of boundary markers shall also be autonomized, involuntary damage shall also be incriminated and prohibited burning and types of arson shall be defined as typical crimes concerning special damage due to the means and danger represented with the consequences arising from such conduct; ttt) To protect patrimony in general with these common incriminations, such as fraud, extortion, receiving and bankruptcy or insolvency, but especially computer fraud and various harmful and abusive administration, looking at new needs arising in commerce with new technologies and looking at the responsibility which must be demanded from persons managing the goods or patrimony of another person and not acting correctly in such administration; uuu) In modern and democratic society justice and the exercise of justice are also goods which must receive penal protection; thus punishment must be consecrated for falsity of procedural acts, for various obstructions to jurisdictional activity, non-exercise of jurisdictional activity when it constitutes denial of justice and violation of the secrecy of justice; vvv) To also include incrimination for acts constituting bribery, prevarication by magistrate or official and lawyer or public defender, and also other activity concerning personal favoritism in justice, together

with classic incriminations such as calumnious denunciation, simulation of crime or non-participation; www) Together with the references set out in the last two numbers, conduct must also be criminalized which, in the exercise of public function, constitutes corruption, embezzlement, abuse of power or public force or economic participation in business by persons exercising public office or function; xxx) In an autonomous title conduct constituting falsification of documents, technical notation, currency and sealed or stamped values, weights and measures, marks, stamps and seals must be prevented with adequate incriminating norms, with their various sanctions according to the nature of the falsified object, probative or fiduciary value and public use or destination, and to provide for the possibility of seizure and forfeiture of objects intended to commit these crimes; yyy) In the area of the economy acts constituting fiscal fraud or smuggling and evasion of duties concerning customs or border matters shall also be incriminated, together with criminal punishment for disobedience to requisition ordered by the government and conduct which may disturb, prejudice or prevent the realization of any public act, such as public tender or judicial auction. Article 3
Articulation with the Code of Criminal Procedure

The Criminal Code to which this legislative authorization refers must articulate its moment of coming into force with the Code of Criminal Procedure and regulatory legislation concerning penal matters in force for a long time, in order to guarantee legal security and coherence in the respective legal order. Article 4 Duration and extension

The legislative authorization given by this law is in force for 180 days, beginning at the moment this law comes into force. Article 5 Coming into force

This legal diploma comes into force on the day following its publication. Approved on 23 September 2008

President of the National Parliament in exercise Maria da Paixão de Jesus da Costa

Promulgated on 7 October 2008 Let it be published. President of the Republic Dr. José Ramos Horta

Law 6/2009, of 15 July (First amendment, as parliamentary appreciation, to the Criminal Code approved by Decree-Law 19/2009, of 8 April)

Life must receive protection beginning immediately from the conception of the human being. Having accepted this principle, we cannot forget that there are also situations which may justify interruption of pregnancy. However, because the goods in conflict here are very serious, the good of the pregnant woman's life and the good of the fetus or embryo's life, both with equal value, only in extreme situations can we consider such interruption legitimate: when with the sacrifice of one life another life can be saved and both lives cannot be held at the same time. Thus, it must be established that interruption of pregnancy may exist only when with such interruption the pregnant woman will not die, and such woman shall decide, according to her conscience, concerning such interruption. Thus, according to article 92, no.1, and 98, the National Parliament decrees, to have the force of law, the following:

Article 1 Amendment to the Penal Code

Article 141 of the Penal Code approved by Decree-Law 19/2009, of 8 April, and annexed to that diploma, is amended as follows:

"Article 141 (…)

1. (…)
2. (…)
3. (…)
4. The provision of the preceding number does not apply when, according to existing medical knowledge and experience and having done everything possible to save the life of the pregnant woman and

the fetus or embryo, the interruption of pregnancy is the only way to avert death from the pregnant woman, namely with medical certification and performed by a doctor or other health professional, under the direction of a doctor, in a public or officially recognized health establishment, and with the consent of the pregnant woman. 5. Before performing the interruption a panel of three doctors must certify the circumstances referred to in the preceding number, in a written and signed medical certificate; this panel shall include the doctor who performs or supervises the performance of the interruption of pregnancy. 6. The consent of the pregnant woman shall be given personally in a written and signed document, having heard, when possible, her spouse, or the person with whom she lives as if married, or another person, at the request of that woman, who shall give it, and, when possible, shall be given at least two days before performing the interruption. 7. When the pregnant woman is a minor, her legal representative shall give consent. 8. When the pregnant woman is an adult or emancipated person who is psychically incapacitated or a person suffering from psychic incapacity, her spouse or the person with whom she lives as if married, or her legal representative, ascendant or descendant, or, failing that, any collateral relative, respectively and successively, shall give this consent. 9. When it is not possible to obtain the medical certificate referred to in no. 5 and/or it is not possible to obtain consent as written in
nos. 6 to 8, and the interruption must be performed urgently and cannot be delayed any longer, the doctor, having regard to each situation, shall decide, also using, when possible, the opinion of other doctors. 10. The panel referred to in no. 5 shall include a doctor who has adequate knowledge to evaluate the circumstances that justify the interruption of pregnancy, and, when possible, a gynecologist/obstetrician. 11. The doctors who issue the medical certificate and the doctors who perform or supervise the performance of the interruption of pregnancy must provide the necessary clarifications to the pregnant woman and, when applicable, to the persons referred to in nos. 7 and 8, namely regarding the method to be used in the interruption, the

effects of the interruption itself and its consequences for the physical and psychic health of the pregnant woman. 12. Doctors and other health professionals have the right to present a conscientious objection to any act relating to the interruption of pregnancy. 13. Doctors and other health professionals who invoke conscientious objection must ensure the immediate intervention of another health professional in the acts that must be performed and also the immediate accompaniment of the pregnant woman. 14. Conscientious objection must be expressed explicitly and must be communicated to the clinical head of the health establishment where the objector works". Article 2 Entry into force

This diploma enters into force on the day following its publication. Approved on 26 May 2009

President of the National Parliament Fernando La Sama de Araújo

Promulgated on 3 July 2009 Published by

President of the Republic José Ramos Horta
